FILE: src\app\App.tsx

import { Route, Routes } from 'react-router-dom';
import { HomePage } from '../pages/home';
import { GameHubPage } from '../pages/game-hub';
import { PublicPlayerPage } from '../pages/public-player';
import { TermsPage } from '../pages/terms';
import { PrivacyPage } from '../pages/privacy';
import { CookiesPage } from '../pages/cookies';
import { RegisterPage } from '../pages/register';
import { LoginPage } from '../pages/login';
import { VerifyPage } from '../pages/verify';
import { VerifyLocationPage } from '../pages/verify-location';
import { ForgotPasswordPage } from '../pages/forgot-password';
import { ResetPasswordPage } from '../pages/reset-password';
import { AccountPage } from '../pages/account';
import { SupportPage } from '../pages/support';
import { TicketPage } from '../pages/ticket';
import { AdminTicketsLayout } from '../widgets/admin-tickets-layout';
import { AdminTicketsPlaceholder } from '../pages/admin-tickets-placeholder';
import { AdminTicketPage } from '../pages/admin-ticket';
import { AccountSettingsPage } from '../pages/account-settings';
import { PlayerProfilePage } from '../pages/player-profile';
import { AdminUsersPage } from '../pages/admin-users';
import { AdminAuditPage } from '../pages/admin-audit';
import { ProtectedRoute } from '../shared/auth/ProtectedRoute';
import { GuestOnlyRoute } from '../shared/auth/GuestOnlyRoute';
import { CommandPalette } from '../widgets/command-palette';
import { CookieConsentBanner } from '../widgets/cookie-consent';

// Архитектура роутинга:
//   PUBLIC  — /, /:game, /player/:game/:riotId, /terms, /privacy, /cookies,
//             /support, /support/ticket/:token
//   GUEST   — /register, /login  (если залогинен — редирект в /account)
//   AUTHED  — /account, /account/settings, /account/riot-profile
//   ADMIN   — /admin/*
//
// Главная (/) больше не GuestOnlyRoute — это публичная витрина трекера.
// Залогиненный пользователь может спокойно смотреть её, как и все.
export function App() {
    return (
        <>
            <CommandPalette />
            <CookieConsentBanner />
            <Routes>
                {/* === ПУБЛИЧНЫЕ: витрина + игровые страницы + профили === */}
                <Route path="/" element={<HomePage />} />
                <Route path="/:slug" element={<GameHubPage />} />
                <Route path="/player/:game/:riotId" element={<PublicPlayerPage />} />

                {/* === ЮРИДИЧЕСКОЕ (публичное) === */}
                <Route path="/terms" element={<TermsPage />} />
                <Route path="/privacy" element={<PrivacyPage />} />
                <Route path="/cookies" element={<CookiesPage />} />
                <Route path="/verify" element={<VerifyPage />} />
                <Route path="/verify-location" element={<VerifyLocationPage />} />
                <Route path="/forgot-password" element={<ForgotPasswordPage />} />
                <Route path="/reset-password" element={<ResetPasswordPage />} />
                <Route path="/support" element={<SupportPage />} />
                <Route path="/support/ticket/:token" element={<TicketPage />} />

                {/* === GUEST-ONLY: если залогинен — на /account === */}
                <Route
                    path="/register"
                    element={
                        <GuestOnlyRoute>
                            <RegisterPage />
                        </GuestOnlyRoute>
                    }
                />
                <Route
                    path="/login"
                    element={
                        <GuestOnlyRoute>
                            <LoginPage />
                        </GuestOnlyRoute>
                    }
                />

                {/* === AUTHED === */}
                <Route
                    path="/account"
                    element={
                        <ProtectedRoute>
                            <AccountPage />
                        </ProtectedRoute>
                    }
                />
                <Route
                    path="/account/settings"
                    element={
                        <ProtectedRoute>
                            <AccountSettingsPage />
                        </ProtectedRoute>
                    }
                />
                <Route
                    path="/account/riot-profile"
                    element={
                        <ProtectedRoute>
                            <PlayerProfilePage />
                        </ProtectedRoute>
                    }
                />

                {/* === ADMIN === */}
                <Route
                    path="/admin/users"
                    element={
                        <ProtectedRoute adminOnly>
                            <AdminUsersPage />
                        </ProtectedRoute>
                    }
                />
                <Route
                    path="/admin/audit"
                    element={
                        <ProtectedRoute adminOnly>
                            <AdminAuditPage />
                        </ProtectedRoute>
                    }
                />
                <Route
                    path="/admin/tickets"
                    element={
                        <ProtectedRoute adminOnly>
                            <AdminTicketsLayout />
                        </ProtectedRoute>
                    }
                >
                    <Route index element={<AdminTicketsPlaceholder />} />
                    <Route path=":number" element={<AdminTicketPage />} />
                </Route>
            </Routes>
        </>
    );
}

FILE: src\features\active-sessions\index.tsx
============================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Panel } from '../../shared/ui/Panel';
import { getLoginHistory, revokeSession, type LoginEvent } from '../../shared/api/statsAuth';
import styles from './index.module.scss';

// "Sign out" здесь реально отзывает сессию на сервере (см. revokeSession в
// shared/api/statsAuth) — сессии теперь не stateless-cookie, а запись в
// серверном реестре (stats_sessions), которую можно отозвать. Записи из
// истории старше этой функции (session_id пуст на бэкенде) по-прежнему
// просто пропадают из списка — отзывать у них нечего, но это не ошибка.
export function ActiveSessions() {
    const [events, setEvents] = useState<LoginEvent[] | null>(null);
    const [pendingId, setPendingId] = useState<string | null>(null);

    useEffect(() => {
        getLoginHistory().then(setEvents);
    }, []);

    async function handleSignOut(id: string) {
        setPendingId(id);
        try {
            await revokeSession(id);
            setEvents(prev => (prev ? prev.filter(e => e.id !== id) : prev));
        } catch {
            alert('Не удалось выйти из этой сессии');
        } finally {
            setPendingId(null);
        }
    }

    function formatLocation(event: LoginEvent): string {
        if (!event.country) return 'Unknown location';
        return event.city ? `${event.city}, ${event.country}` : event.country;
    }

    return (
        <Panel
            title="Recent Sign-ins"
            subtitle="Devices and locations that have recently signed in to your account. Sign out a device to immediately revoke its access."
        >
            {events === null && <p className={styles.hint}>Loading…</p>}
            {events?.length === 0 && <p className={styles.hint}>No recent sign-ins.</p>}
            {events && events.length > 0 && (
                <ul className={styles.list}>
                    {events.map((event, index) => (
                        <li key={event.id} className={styles.item}>
                            <div className={styles.icon}>💻</div>
                            <div className={styles.info}>
                                <div className={styles.device}>
                                    {event.device || 'Unknown device'}
                                    {index === 0 && <span className={styles.badge}>Most recent</span>}
                                </div>
                                <div className={styles.meta}>
                                    {formatLocation(event)} {event.ip && `• IP: ${event.ip}`}
                                </div>
                            </div>
                            <button
                                className={styles.btn}
                                onClick={() => handleSignOut(event.id)}
                                disabled={pendingId === event.id}
                            >
                                {pendingId === event.id ? 'Signing out…' : 'Sign out'}
                            </button>
                        </li>
                    ))}
                </ul>
            )}
        </Panel>
    );
}

FILE: src\features\delete-account\index.tsx
============================================================
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Modal } from '../../shared/ui/Modal';
import { TextField } from '../../shared/ui/TextField';
import { PasswordField } from '../../shared/ui/PasswordField';
import { Button } from '../../shared/ui/Button';
import { IconCheck } from '../../shared/ui/Icons';
import { deleteAccount, ApiError } from '../../shared/api/statsAuth';
import styles from './index.module.scss';

const CONFIRM_WORD = 'DELETE';

interface DeleteAccountModalProps {
    open: boolean;
    onClose: () => void;
}

// Единственное по-настоящему необратимое действие во всём приложении —
// остальные опасные действия (бан, разадминивание) обратимы и живут в
// AdminUserController. Подтверждение в два фактора (слово DELETE + пароль)
// и явный список последствий — то, что реально удаляется, не "аккаунт
// исчезнет" общими словами.
export function DeleteAccountModal({ open, onClose }: DeleteAccountModalProps) {
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const [confirmWord, setConfirmWord] = useState('');
    const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
    const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const [pending, setPending] = useState(false);
    const [deleted, setDeleted] = useState(false);

    const canSubmit = confirmWord === CONFIRM_WORD && password.length > 0;

    function handleClose() {
        if (pending) return;
        setConfirmWord('');
        setPassword('');
        setError(null);
        setDeleted(false);
        onClose();
    }

    async function handleDelete() {
        if (!canSubmit) return;
        setPending(true);
        setError(null);
        try {
            await deleteAccount(password);
            setDeleted(true);
            window.setTimeout(() => navigate('/', { replace: true }), 1400);
        } catch (err) {
            setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : 'Something went wrong.');
        } finally {
            setPending(false);
        }
    }

    return (
        <Modal open={open} onClose={handleClose} labelledBy="delete-account-title">
            {deleted ? (
                <div className={styles.deletedState}>
                    <span className={styles.deletedIcon} aria-hidden="true">
                        <IconCheck />
                    </span>
                    <h2 className={styles.deletedTitle}>Account deleted</h2>
                    <p className={styles.deletedText}>Taking you back to the homepage…</p>
                </div>
            ) : (
                <>
                    <h2 className={styles.title} id="delete-account-title">
                        Delete account
                    </h2>
                    <p className={styles.lead}>
                        This action is permanent. All your data, match history, and settings will be deleted.
                    </p>
                    <ul className={styles.consequences}>
                        <li>Your profile, email, and password are permanently removed.</li>
                        <li>Linked Riot and Discord connections are unlinked.</li>
                        <li>Open support tickets are closed and your reply history is deleted.</li>
                        <li>This cannot be undone — there is no recovery period.</li>
                    </ul>

                    {error && <p className={styles.error}>{error}</p>}

                    <div className={styles.field}>
                        <TextField
                            id="delete-confirm-word"
                            label={`Type ${CONFIRM_WORD} to confirm`}
                            value={confirmWord}
                            onChange={e => setConfirmWord(e.target.value)}
                            autoComplete="off"
                        />
                    </div>
                    <div className={styles.field}>
                        <PasswordField
                            id="delete-password"
                            label="Password"
                            autoComplete="current-password"
                            value={password}
                            onChange={setPassword}
                        />
                    </div>

                    <div className={styles.actions}>
                        <Button variant="ghost" onClick={handleClose} disabled={pending}>
                            Cancel
                        </Button>
                        <Button
                            variant="flat"
                            className={styles.deleteBtn}
                            onClick={handleDelete}
                            loading={pending}
                            disabled={!canSubmit}
                        >
                            {pending ? 'Deleting…' : 'Delete account'}
                        </Button>
                    </div>
                </>
            )}
        </Modal>
    );
}

FILE: src\features\linked-accounts\index.tsx
============================================================
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Panel } from '../../shared/ui/Panel';
import { Button } from '../../shared/ui/Button';
import { LinkIcon } from '../../shared/ui/Icons';
import styles from './index.module.scss';

interface LinkedAccountsProps {
    riotLinked: boolean;
}

// Новый блок на странице аккаунта: раньше статус привязки Riot (user.
// riotLinked) нигде в Settings не был виден — только неявно влиял на
// текст в pages/account (hero-заглушка "Waiting for Riot Approval").
// "View Riot Profile" раньше вёл на /riot-lookup, которая намеренно не
// подключена в App.tsx (см. комментарий там про RSO/VALORANT API Pending
// Review) — мёртвая ссылка. Теперь ведёт на собственную страницу профиля
// внутри панели управления (/account/riot-profile), не выдумывая данные,
// которых у бэкенда ещё нет (gameName/tagLine для привязанного аккаунта
// бэкенд не хранит — см. User.java, только riot_puuid).
export function LinkedAccounts({ riotLinked }: LinkedAccountsProps) {
    const navigate = useNavigate();

    return (
        <Panel title="Linked Accounts" subtitle="Connect external accounts to unlock more of Extree Stats.">
            <div className={styles.row}>
                <div className={styles.rowLeft}>
                    <div className={`${styles.iconWrap} ${riotLinked ? styles.iconWrapLinked : ''}`}>
                        <LinkIcon />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <div className={styles.rowTitle}>Riot Games</div>
                        <div className={styles.rowSubtitle}>
                            {riotLinked
                                ? 'Your Riot account is connected.'
                                : 'Link your Riot account to view match stats once Riot approves full API access.'}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div className={styles.rowRight}>
                    {riotLinked ? (
                        <>
                            <span className={styles.connectedBadge}>Connected</span>
                            <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={() => navigate('/account/riot-profile')}>
                                View Riot Profile
                            </Button>
                        </>
                    ) : (
                        <>
                            <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={() => navigate('/account/riot-profile')}>
                                Look up
                            </Button>
                            <Button variant="flat" size="sm" href="/auth/riot/login">
                                Connect
                            </Button>
                        </>
                    )}
                </div>
            </div>
        </Panel>
    );
}

FILE: src\features\riot-sign-in\RiotSignInButton\index.tsx
============================================================
import { useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Button } from '../../../shared/ui/Button';
import styles from './index.module.scss';

// Настоящая ссылка на stats-backend (см. stats-backend/server.js в корне
// репозитория) — GET /auth/riot/login. RIOT_CLIENT_ID на бэкенде пока не
// задан нигде (Riot ещё не выдал RSO-доступ — заявка на продукт сама
// Pending Review, а RSO Riot открывает отдельным приглашением уже после
// одобрения), поэтому клик реально доходит до настоящего сервера и
// честно возвращается на "/?auth_error=1" вместо похода в Riot с пустым
// client_id (см. stats-backend/server.js, /auth/riot/login) — здесь это
// читается через react-router-dom и показывается статусной заметкой,
// вместо имитации рабочего флоу. Как только Riot выдаст client_id/
// client_secret — ничего в этом компоненте менять не нужно, достаточно
// прописать их в .env на сервере.
interface RiotSignInButtonProps {
    // Страницы, где эта кнопка встречается больше одного раза (напр. лендинг
    // повторяет её в hero и в финальном CTA-блоке), должны передать разный
    // noteId — иначе на странице окажется два элемента с одинаковым id, что
    // ломает aria-describedby.
    noteId?: string;
}

export function RiotSignInButton({ noteId = 'riot-sign-in-note' }: RiotSignInButtonProps) {
    const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
    const hasAuthError = searchParams.get('auth_error') === '1';

    return (
        <div>
            <Button variant="primary" href="/auth/riot/login" aria-describedby={noteId}>
                Sign in with Riot
            </Button>
            {hasAuthError && (
                <p id={noteId} className={styles.note} role="status">
                    Riot Sign-On isn't live yet — this product is still in review with Riot Games.
                </p>
            )}
        </div>
    );
}

FILE: src\features\security-score\index.tsx
============================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { IconCheck, IconX } from '../../shared/ui/Icons';
import { Button } from '../../shared/ui/Button';
import type { AccountUser } from '../../shared/api/statsAuth';
import styles from './index.module.scss';

const RADIUS = 54;
const CIRCUMFERENCE = 2 * Math.PI * RADIUS;

interface Check {
    label: string;
    detail: string;
    done: boolean;
}

interface SecurityScoreProps {
    user: Pick<AccountUser, 'emailVerified' | 'isTwoFactorEnabled' | 'riotLinked'>;
    onEnable2fa: () => void;
}

// Единственный источник "уровня защиты" аккаунта на странице — сводит в
// одну цифру то, что иначе разбросано по отдельным панелям ниже (email
// verified в Personal Information, 2FA в своей секции), и даёт один клик
// до самого weighty действия (включить 2FA), а не "иди ищи сам". Очки
// считаются только из того, что уже есть в AccountUser — ни одного нового
// запроса к бэкенду.
export function SecurityScore({ user, onEnable2fa }: SecurityScoreProps) {
    const checks: Check[] = [
        {
            label: 'Email verified',
            detail: "Confirms it's really you and lets us reach you about account activity.",
            done: user.emailVerified,
        },
        {
            label: 'Two-factor authentication',
            detail: 'An authenticator app code is required on every new sign-in.',
            done: user.isTwoFactorEnabled,
        },
    ];
    const score = Math.round((checks.filter(c => c.done).length / checks.length) * 100);

    const [displayScore, setDisplayScore] = useState(0);
    useEffect(() => {
        const raf = requestAnimationFrame(() => setDisplayScore(score));
        return () => cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
    }, [score]);

    const tier = score === 100 ? 'max' : score >= 50 ? 'mid' : 'low';
    const offset = CIRCUMFERENCE * (1 - displayScore / 100);

    return (
        <section className={`${styles.card} ${styles[tier]}`}>
            <div className={styles.ring}>
                <svg viewBox="0 0 120 120" className={styles.ringSvg}>
                    <circle className={styles.ringTrack} cx="60" cy="60" r={RADIUS} />
                    <circle
                        className={styles.ringFill}
                        cx="60"
                        cy="60"
                        r={RADIUS}
                        strokeDasharray={CIRCUMFERENCE}
                        strokeDashoffset={offset}
                    />
                </svg>
                <div className={styles.ringLabel}>
                    <span className={styles.ringScore}>
                        {displayScore}
                        <span className={styles.ringUnit}>%</span>
                    </span>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div className={styles.body}>
                <h3 className={styles.heading}>{score === 100 ? 'Your account is fully secured' : 'Security score'}</h3>
                <p className={styles.subheading}>
                    {score === 100
                        ? 'Email verified and two-factor authentication enabled — nice work.'
                        : 'A couple of quick steps stand between you and a fully locked-down account.'}
                </p>

                <ul className={styles.checklist}>
                    {checks.map(c => (
                        <li key={c.label} className={`${styles.checkItem} ${c.done ? styles.checkDone : ''}`}>
                            <span className={styles.checkIcon}>{c.done ? <IconCheck /> : <IconX />}</span>
                            <div>
                                <div className={styles.checkLabel}>{c.label}</div>
                                <div className={styles.checkDetail}>{c.detail}</div>
                            </div>
                        </li>
                    ))}
                </ul>

                {!user.isTwoFactorEnabled && (
                    <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={onEnable2fa}>
                        Enable 2FA now
                    </Button>
                )}

                {user.riotLinked && <div className={styles.riotBadge}>Riot account linked</div>}
            </div>
        </section>
    );
}

FILE: src\features\update-email\index.tsx
============================================================
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { Panel } from '../../shared/ui/Panel';
import { TextField } from '../../shared/ui/TextField';
import { Button } from '../../shared/ui/Button';
import { CodeInput } from '../../shared/ui/CodeInput';
import { useToast } from '../../shared/ui/Toast/useToast';
import { requestEmailChange, confirmEmailChange, ApiError } from '../../shared/api/statsAuth';
import styles from './index.module.scss';

const CODE_LENGTH = 6;
const EMAIL_RE = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;

export function UpdateEmail({ email, onChanged }: { email: string; onChanged: (newEmail: string) => void }) {
    const { showToast } = useToast();
    const [step, setStep] = useState<'form' | 'code'>('form');
    const [currentPassword, setCurrentPassword] = useState('');
    const [newEmail, setNewEmail] = useState('');
    const [emailTouched, setEmailTouched] = useState(false);
    const [code, setCode] = useState('');
    const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const [pending, setPending] = useState(false);

    const emailInvalid = emailTouched && newEmail.length > 0 && !EMAIL_RE.test(newEmail);

    async function onRequest(e: FormEvent) {
        e.preventDefault();
        setError(null);
        setPending(true);
        try {
            await requestEmailChange(currentPassword, newEmail);
            setStep('code');
        } catch (err) {
            setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : 'Something went wrong.');
        } finally {
            setPending(false);
        }
    }

    async function onConfirm(e: FormEvent) {
        e.preventDefault();
        setError(null);
        setPending(true);
        try {
            const result = await confirmEmailChange(code);
            onChanged(result.user.email);
            setStep('form');
            setCurrentPassword('');
            setNewEmail('');
            setEmailTouched(false);
            setCode('');
            showToast('Email updated.', 'success');
        } catch (err) {
            setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : 'Something went wrong.');
        } finally {
            setPending(false);
        }
    }

    return (
        <Panel title="Change email">
            {error && <p className={styles.error}>{error}</p>}
            {step === 'form' ? (
                <form className={styles.form} onSubmit={onRequest}>
                    <TextField
                        id="email-pass"
                        label="Current password"
                        type="password"
                        required
                        value={currentPassword}
                        onChange={e => setCurrentPassword(e.target.value)}
                    />
                    <TextField
                        id="email-new"
                        label="New email"
                        type="email"
                        placeholder={email}
                        required
                        error={emailInvalid}
                        value={newEmail}
                        onChange={e => setNewEmail(e.target.value)}
                        onBlur={() => setEmailTouched(true)}
                    />
                    {emailInvalid && <p className={styles.fieldError}>Enter a valid email address.</p>}
                    <div className={styles.actions}>
                        <Button type="submit" variant="flat" loading={pending} disabled={emailInvalid}>
                            {pending ? 'Sending code…' : 'Send confirmation code'}
                        </Button>
                    </div>
                </form>
            ) : (
                <form className={styles.form} onSubmit={onConfirm}>
                    <p className={styles.hint}>
                        We sent a 6-digit code to <strong>{newEmail}</strong>.
                    </p>
                    <CodeInput length={CODE_LENGTH} value={code} onChange={setCode} disabled={pending} autoFocus />
                    <div className={styles.actions}>
                        <Button type="submit" variant="flat" loading={pending} disabled={code.length < CODE_LENGTH}>
                            {pending ? 'Confirming…' : 'Confirm new email'}
                        </Button>
                    </div>
                    <button type="button" className={styles.linkBtn} onClick={() => setStep('form')}>
                        Cancel
                    </button>
                </form>
            )}
        </Panel>
    );
}

FILE: src\features\update-password\index.tsx
============================================================
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { Panel } from '../../shared/ui/Panel';
import { TextField } from '../../shared/ui/TextField';
import { Button } from '../../shared/ui/Button';
import { PasswordRequirements } from '../../shared/ui/PasswordRequirements';
import { isPasswordValid } from '../../shared/lib/passwordRules';
import { changePassword, ApiError } from '../../shared/api/statsAuth';
import styles from './index.module.scss';

export function UpdatePassword() {
    const [currentPassword, setCurrentPassword] = useState('');
    const [newPassword, setNewPassword] = useState('');
    const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState('');
    const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const [success, setSuccess] = useState(false);
    const [pending, setPending] = useState(false);

    const passwordsMatch = confirmPassword.length === 0 || confirmPassword === newPassword;
    const canSubmit = currentPassword.length > 0 && isPasswordValid(newPassword) && confirmPassword === newPassword;

    async function onSubmit(e: FormEvent) {
        e.preventDefault();
        setError(null);
        setSuccess(false);
        setPending(true);
        try {
            await changePassword(currentPassword, newPassword);
            setCurrentPassword('');
            setNewPassword('');
            setConfirmPassword('');
            setSuccess(true);
        } catch (err) {
            setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : 'Something went wrong.');
        } finally {
            setPending(false);
        }
    }

    return (
        <Panel title="Change password">
            {error && <p className={styles.error}>{error}</p>}
            {success && <p className={styles.notice}>Password updated.</p>}
            <form className={styles.form} onSubmit={onSubmit}>
                <TextField
                    id="pass-cur"
                    label="Current password"
                    type="password"
                    required
                    value={currentPassword}
                    onChange={e => setCurrentPassword(e.target.value)}
                />
                <div className={styles.passField}>
                    <TextField
                        id="pass-new"
                        label="New password"
                        type="password"
                        required
                        value={newPassword}
                        onChange={e => setNewPassword(e.target.value)}
                    />
                    <PasswordRequirements password={newPassword} />
                </div>
                <div>
                    <TextField
                        id="pass-conf"
                        label="Repeat new password"
                        type="password"
                        required
                        value={confirmPassword}
                        onChange={e => setConfirmPassword(e.target.value)}
                    />
                    {!passwordsMatch && <p className={styles.mismatch}>Passwords don't match.</p>}
                </div>
                <Button type="submit" variant="flat" fullWidth disabled={pending || !canSubmit}>
                    {pending ? 'Updating…' : 'Update password'}
                </Button>
            </form>
        </Panel>
    );
}

============================================================
FILE: src\main.tsx
============================================================
import { StrictMode } from 'react';
import { createRoot } from 'react-dom/client';
import { BrowserRouter } from 'react-router-dom';
import * as Sentry from '@sentry/react';
import { App } from './app/App';
import { AuthProvider } from './shared/auth/AuthProvider';
import { ConsentProvider } from './shared/consent/ConsentProvider';
import { ToastProvider } from './shared/ui/Toast/ToastProvider';
import './app/styles/global.css';

// Sentry.init() больше не вызывается здесь безусловно — только из
// shared/consent/thirdParty.ts (enableSentry), и только после согласия на
// категорию "Error monitoring" (или сразу, если согласие уже было дано в
// прошлый визит — см. ConsentProvider). <Sentry.ErrorBoundary> безопасно
// смонтирован и без вызванного init(): captureException внутри него
// становится no-op, пока клиент не создан, но сам fallback UI на ошибке
// отрисуется как обычно.

createRoot(document.getElementById('root')!).render(
    <StrictMode>
        <Sentry.ErrorBoundary
            fallback={() => (
                <div style={{ padding: 40, textAlign: 'center', fontFamily: 'sans-serif' }}>
                    Something went wrong. Please refresh the page.
                </div>
            )}
        >
            <BrowserRouter>
                <ConsentProvider>
                    <ToastProvider>
                        <AuthProvider>
                            <App />
                        </AuthProvider>
                    </ToastProvider>
                </ConsentProvider>
            </BrowserRouter>
        </Sentry.ErrorBoundary>
    </StrictMode>
);

============================================================

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useNavigate, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { DashboardShell } from '../../widgets/dashboard-shell';
import { Panel } from '../../shared/ui/Panel';
import { Button } from '../../shared/ui/Button';
import { Modal } from '../../shared/ui/Modal';
import { ProfileAvatar } from '../../shared/ui/ProfileAvatar';
import {
    getAccount,
    setup2fa,
    enable2fa,
    disable2fa,
    regenerateRecoveryCodes,
    type AccountUser,
} from '../../shared/api/statsAuth';
import { UpdateEmail } from '../../features/update-email';
import { UpdatePassword } from '../../features/update-password';
import { ActiveSessions } from '../../features/active-sessions';
import { SecurityScore } from '../../features/security-score';
import { LinkedAccounts } from '../../features/linked-accounts';
import { DeleteAccountModal } from '../../features/delete-account';
import { useToast } from '../../shared/ui/Toast/useToast';
import { Select } from '../../shared/ui/Select';
import styles from './index.module.scss';
import { VerifiedBadge } from '../../shared/ui/VerifiedBadge';
import { TextField } from '../../shared/ui/TextField';
import { PasswordField } from '../../shared/ui/PasswordField';
import { CodeInput } from '../../shared/ui/CodeInput';
import { ShieldIcon, BellIcon, IconCheck, IconX } from '../../shared/ui/Icons';
import { Skeleton } from '../../shared/ui/Skeleton';
import { EmptyState } from '../../shared/ui/EmptyState';
import { QRCodeSVG } from 'qrcode.react';

function SettingsSkeleton() {
    return (
        <div className={styles.page}>
            <Skeleton width={140} height={14} className={styles.skeletonMb16} />
            <Skeleton width={100} height={28} className={styles.skeletonMb24} />
            <div className={styles.layoutContent}>
                <div className={styles.panel}>
                    <div className={styles.skeletonRow}>
                        <Skeleton width={64} height={64} radius="50%" />
                        <div className={styles.skeletonCol}>
                            <Skeleton width={160} height={20} />
                            <Skeleton width={200} height={14} />
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
}

function AccountStatusCard({ user }: { user: AccountUser }) {
    return (
        <Panel title="Account status">
            <div className={styles.statusList}>
                <div className={styles.statusRow}>
                    <span className={styles.label}>Role</span>
                    <span className={user.isAdmin ? styles.pillAdmin : styles.pillNeutral}>
                        {user.isAdmin ? 'Admin' : 'Standard'}
                    </span>
                </div>
                <div className={styles.statusRow}>
                    <span className={styles.label}>Email</span>
                    <span className={user.emailVerified ? styles.pillGood : styles.pillWarn}>
                        {user.emailVerified ? <><IconCheck /> Verified</> : <><IconX /> Unverified</>}
                    </span>
                </div>
                <div className={styles.statusRow}>
                    <span className={styles.label}>Riot account</span>
                    <span className={user.riotLinked ? styles.pillGood : styles.pillNeutral}>
                        {user.riotLinked ? 'Linked' : 'Not linked'}
                    </span>
                </div>
                <div className={styles.statusRow}>
                    <span className={styles.label}>2FA</span>
                    <span className={user.isTwoFactorEnabled ? styles.pillGood : styles.pillNeutral}>
                        {user.isTwoFactorEnabled ? 'Enabled' : 'Disabled'}
                    </span>
                </div>
            </div>
        </Panel>
    );
}

export function AccountSettingsPage() {
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const { showToast } = useToast();
    const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
    
    // Читаем активную вкладку из URL (profile по умолчанию)
    const tabParam = searchParams.get('tab') as 'profile' | 'security' | 'notifications' | null;
    const activeTab = tabParam || 'profile';

    const [user, setUser] = useState<AccountUser | null>(null);
    const [isEditingProfile, setIsEditingProfile] = useState(false);
    const [banned, setBanned] = useState(false);
    const [twoFaModalOpen, setTwoFaModalOpen] = useState(false);
    const [deleteModalOpen, setDeleteModalOpen] = useState(false);
    const [totpSetup, setTotpSetup] = useState<{ secret: string; otpauthUrl: string; recoveryCodes: string[] } | null>(null);
    const [totpCode, setTotpCode] = useState('');
    const [totpPending, setTotpPending] = useState(false);
    const [totpError, setTotpError] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const [setupPassword, setSetupPassword] = useState('');
    const [setupPending, setSetupPending] = useState(false);

    useEffect(() => {
        getAccount().then(result => {
            if (result.banned) {
                setBanned(true);
            } else if (!result.user) {
                navigate('/login');
            } else {
                setUser(result.user);
            }
        });
    }, [navigate]);

    function handleCloseTwoFaModal() {
        setTwoFaModalOpen(false);
        setTotpSetup(null);
        setTotpCode('');
        setSetupPassword('');
        setTotpError(null);
    }

    async function handleSetup2fa() {
        if (!setupPassword) return;
        setSetupPending(true);
        setTotpError(null);
        try {
            const r = await setup2fa(setupPassword);
            setTotpSetup(r);
        } catch (e) {
            setTotpError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Failed to start 2FA setup');
            setSetupPassword('');
        } finally {
            setSetupPending(false);
        }
    }

    async function handleEnable2fa() {
        if (!totpCode.trim()) return;
        setTotpPending(true);
        setTotpError(null);
        try {
            await enable2fa(totpCode.trim(), setupPassword);
            const fresh = await getAccount();
            if (fresh.user) setUser(fresh.user);
            handleCloseTwoFaModal();
            showToast('2FA enabled. Your account is now fully secured.', 'success');
        } catch (e) {
            setTotpError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Failed to enable 2FA');
        } finally {
            setTotpPending(false);
        }
    }

    async function handleDisable2fa() {
        const pwd = prompt('Enter your password to disable 2FA:');
        if (!pwd) return;
        try {
            await disable2fa(pwd);
            const fresh = await getAccount();
            if (fresh.user) setUser(fresh.user);
            showToast('2FA disabled.', 'success');
        } catch (e) {
            showToast(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Failed to disable 2FA', 'error');
        }
    }

    async function handleRegenCodes() {
        const pwd = prompt('Enter your password to regenerate recovery codes:');
        if (!pwd) return;
        try {
            const r = await regenerateRecoveryCodes(pwd);
            alert('New recovery codes (save them):\n\n' + r.recoveryCodes.join('\n'));
            showToast('New recovery codes generated.', 'success');
        } catch (e) {
            showToast(e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Failed to regenerate codes', 'error');
        }
    }

    if (banned) {
        return (
            <DashboardShell user={null} title="Account banned">
                <div style={{ padding: 40, textAlign: 'center' }}>
                    <h2>Your account has been banned.</h2>
                    <p>Please contact <a href="mailto:support@extree.tech">support@extree.tech</a>.</p>
                </div>
            </DashboardShell>
        );
    }

    if (!user) {
        return (
            <DashboardShell user={null} title="" wide>
                <SettingsSkeleton />
            </DashboardShell>
        );
    }

    return (
        <DashboardShell user={{ email: user.email, isAdmin: user.isAdmin }} title="" wide>
            <div className={styles.page}>
                <Link to="/account" className={styles.backBtn}>
                    ← Back to Dashboard
                </Link>
                <h1 className={styles.title}>Settings</h1>

                <div className={styles.layoutContent}>
                    <main>
                        {activeTab === 'profile' && (
                            <div className={styles.tabContent}>
                                <Panel title="Personal Information">
                                    {isEditingProfile ? (
                                        <>
                                            <div className={styles.avatarSection}>
                                                <ProfileAvatar
                                                    currentUrl={user.avatarUrl}
                                                    email={user.email}
                                                    onUploaded={url => setUser(u => u && { ...u, avatarUrl: url })}
                                                />
                                                <div className={styles.avatarInfo}>
                                                    <p className={styles.hintText}>JPG, PNG or GIF. Max 2MB.</p>
                                                </div>
                                            </div>
                                            <div className={styles.infoList}>
                                                <div className={styles.row}>
                                                    <span className={styles.label}>Name</span>
                                                    <div style={{ width: '60%' }}>
                                                        <TextField
                                                            id="profile-name"
                                                            label=""
                                                            value={user.name || ''}
                                                            onChange={e => setUser(u => u && { ...u, name: e.target.value })}
                                                        />
                                                    </div>
                                                </div>
                                                <div className={styles.row}>
                                                    <span className={styles.label}>Nickname</span>
                                                    <div style={{ width: '60%' }}>
                                                        <TextField
                                                            id="profile-nickname"
                                                            label=""
                                                            value={user.nickname || ''}
                                                            onChange={e => setUser(u => u && { ...u, nickname: e.target.value })}
                                                        />
                                                    </div>
                                                </div>
                                                <div className={styles.row}>
                                                    <span className={styles.label}>Email</span>
                                                    <span className={styles.value}>{user.email}</span>
                                                </div>
                                                <div className={styles.row}>
                                                    <span className={styles.label}>Language</span>
                                                    <div style={{ width: '180px' }}>
                                                        <Select
                                                            options={[
                                                                { value: 'en', label: 'English (US)' },
                                                                { value: 'ru', label: 'Русский' },
                                                            ]}
                                                            value="en"
                                                            onChange={val => console.log('Язык изменен на', val)}
                                                        />
                                                    </div>
                                                </div>
                                            </div>
                                            <div className={styles.profileActions}>
                                                <Button variant="flat" onClick={() => setIsEditingProfile(false)}>
                                                    Save
                                                </Button>
                                                <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setIsEditingProfile(false)}>
                                                    Cancel
                                                </Button>
                                            </div>
                                        </>
                                    ) : (
                                        <>
                                            <div className={styles.viewHeader}>
                                                <ProfileAvatar
                                                    currentUrl={user.avatarUrl}
                                                    email={user.email}
                                                    onUploaded={url => setUser(u => u && { ...u, avatarUrl: url })}
                                                />
                                                <div className={styles.viewHeaderText}>
                                                    <div className={styles.viewName}>
                                                        {user.name || '—'}
                                                        {user.isVerified && <VerifiedBadge />}
                                                    </div>
                                                    <div className={styles.viewEmail}>
                                                        {user.email}
                                                        {user.emailVerified ? (
                                                            <span className={styles.badgeSuccess}>Verified</span>
                                                        ) : (
                                                            <span className={styles.badgeWarning}>Unverified</span>
                                                        )}
                                                    </div>
                                                </div>
                                            </div>
                                            <div className={styles.profileActions}>
                                                <Button variant="flat" onClick={() => setIsEditingProfile(true)}>
                                                    Edit profile
                                                </Button>
                                            </div>
                                        </>
                                    )}
                                </Panel>
                                <UpdateEmail
                                    email={user.email}
                                    onChanged={newEmail => setUser(u => u && { ...u, email: newEmail })}
                                />
                                <LinkedAccounts riotLinked={user.riotLinked} />
                            </div>
                        )}

                        {activeTab === 'security' && (
                            <div className={styles.tabContent}>
                                <div className={styles.securityGrid}>
                                    <SecurityScore user={user} onEnable2fa={() => setTwoFaModalOpen(true)} />
                                    <AccountStatusCard user={user} />
                                </div>

                                <div className={styles.securityGrid}>
                                    <Panel title="Two-factor authentication (2FA)">
                                        {user.isTwoFactorEnabled ? (
                                            <>
                                                <div className={styles.enabledBanner}>
                                                    <ShieldIcon />
                                                    <div>
                                                        <strong>2FA is enabled.</strong> You'll be asked for a code from your authenticator app on every sign-in.
                                                    </div>
                                                </div>
                                                <div className={styles.totpActions}>
                                                    <Button variant="ghost" onClick={handleDisable2fa}>Disable 2FA</Button>
                                                    <Button variant="ghost" onClick={handleRegenCodes}>New recovery codes</Button>
                                                </div>
                                            </>
                                        ) : (
                                            <div className={styles.twoFaCta}>
                                                <span className={styles.twoFaCtaIcon}><ShieldIcon /></span>
                                                <p className={styles.hintText}>Add an extra layer of security to your account.</p>
                                                <Button variant="flat" onClick={() => setTwoFaModalOpen(true)}>Set up 2FA</Button>
                                            </div>
                                        )}
                                    </Panel>
                                    <UpdatePassword />
                                </div>

                                <ActiveSessions />

                                <Panel title="Danger Zone" className={styles.dangerPanel}>
                                    <div className={styles.dangerRow}>
                                        <div>
                                            <h4>Delete Account</h4>
                                            <p className={styles.hintText}>Once deleted, there is no going back.</p>
                                        </div>
                                        <Button variant="ghost" className={styles.deleteAccountBtn} onClick={() => setDeleteModalOpen(true)}>
                                            Delete Account
                                        </Button>
                                    </div>
                                </Panel>
                            </div>
                        )}

                        {activeTab === 'notifications' && (
                            <div className={styles.tabContent}>
                                <Panel>
                                    <EmptyState
                                        icon={<BellIcon />}
                                        title="Notification preferences aren't available yet"
                                        description="We're still building this out — check back soon."
                                    />
                                </Panel>
                            </div>
                        )}
                    </main>
                </div>
            </div>

            <Modal open={twoFaModalOpen} onClose={handleCloseTwoFaModal} title="Set up two-factor authentication" labelledBy="twofa-modal-title">
                {totpError && <p className={styles.errorBanner}>{totpError}</p>}
                {totpSetup ? (
                    <div className={styles.totpSetup}>
                        <div className={styles.totpStep}>
                            <span className={styles.stepBadge}>1</span>
                            <div>
                                <p className={styles.stepTitle}>Scan with your authenticator app</p>
                                <p className={styles.hintText}>Google Authenticator, Authy, 1Password, or any TOTP app.</p>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        <div className={styles.qrRow}>
                            <div className={styles.qrCard}><QRCodeSVG value={totpSetup.otpauthUrl} size={168} /></div>
                            <div className={styles.secretBlock}>
                                <span className={styles.secretLabel}>Can't scan? Enter this code manually</span>
                                <code className={styles.secretValue}>{totpSetup.secret}</code>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <div className={styles.totpStep}>
                            <span className={styles.stepBadge}>2</span>
                            <div>
                                <p className={styles.stepTitle}>Save your recovery codes</p>
                                <p className={styles.hintText}>Each code works once, if you ever lose access to your authenticator app.</p>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        <div className={styles.recoveryGrid}>
                            {totpSetup.recoveryCodes.map(code => (
                                <code key={code} className={styles.recoveryChip}>{code}</code>
                            ))}
                        </div>

                        <div className={styles.totpStep}>
                            <span className={styles.stepBadge}>3</span>
                            <div><p className={styles.stepTitle}>Enter the 6-digit code from your app</p></div>
                        </div>
                        <div className={styles.codeInputWrap}>
                            <CodeInput value={totpCode} onChange={setTotpCode} autoFocus />
                        </div>

                        <div className={styles.totpActions}>
                            <Button variant="flat" onClick={handleEnable2fa} loading={totpPending} disabled={totpCode.length < 6}>
                                {totpPending ? 'Enabling…' : 'Enable 2FA'}
                            </Button>
                            <Button variant="ghost" onClick={handleCloseTwoFaModal}>Cancel</Button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                ) : (
                    <div>
                        <p className={styles.hintText}>Confirm your password to continue.</p>
                        <div style={{ marginTop: 12, maxWidth: 320 }}>
                            <PasswordField id="setup-2fa-password" label="Confirm your password" autoComplete="current-password" value={setupPassword} onChange={setSetupPassword} />
                        </div>
                        <div className={styles.totpActions} style={{ marginTop: 16 }}>
                            <Button variant="flat" onClick={handleSetup2fa} loading={setupPending} disabled={!setupPassword}>
                                {setupPending ? 'Starting…' : 'Continue'}
                            </Button>
                            <Button variant="ghost" onClick={handleCloseTwoFaModal}>Cancel</Button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                )}
            </Modal>

            <DeleteAccountModal open={deleteModalOpen} onClose={() => setDeleteModalOpen(false)} />
        </DashboardShell>
    );
}

import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { DashboardShell } from '../../widgets/dashboard-shell';
import { Panel } from '../../shared/ui/Panel';
import { Button } from '../../shared/ui/Button';
import { useAuth } from '../../shared/auth/useAuth';
import { Skeleton } from '../../shared/ui/Skeleton';
import styles from './index.module.scss';

// Личный кабинет пользователя — не профиль игрока, а "хаб":
//   - ссылки на настройки/безопасность (там всё 2FA, пароли, сессии)
//   - сохранённые игроки (пока пусто — задел на будущее)
//   - быстрый переход к поиску игрока на публичной витрине
// Публичная статистика живёт в /player/:game/:riotId — там же
// ищутся чужие ники без логина.
export function AccountPage() {
    const { user, banned } = useAuth();

    if (banned) {
        return (
            <DashboardShell user={null} title="Account banned">
                <div style={{ padding: 40, textAlign: 'center' }}>
                    <h2>Your account has been banned.</h2>
                    <p>
                        Please contact <a href="mailto:support@extree.tech">support@extree.tech</a>.
                    </p>
                </div>
            </DashboardShell>
        );
    }

    if (!user) {
        return (
            <DashboardShell user={null} title="">
                <Skeleton width={320} height={34} radius={6} />
            </DashboardShell>
        );
    }

    return (
        <DashboardShell
            user={{ email: user.email, isAdmin: user.isAdmin }}
            title={`Welcome back, ${user.nickname || user.name || user.email.split('@')[0]}`}
            subtitle="Your dashboard — jump into a search or manage your account."
            wide
        >
            <div className={styles.page}>
                <div className={styles.hubGrid}>
                    <Panel title="Public stats">
                        <h3 className={styles.cardTitle}>Look up any player</h3>
                        <p className={styles.cardText}>
                            Search any player across supported games. No login required —
                            public stats live on the site.
                        </p>
                        <div className={styles.cardActions}>
                            <Button href="/" variant="flat" size="sm">
                                Open tracker
                            </Button>
                            <Button href="/valorant" variant="ghost" size="sm">
                                VALORANT
                            </Button>
                        </div>
                    </Panel>

                    <Panel title="Security">
                        <h3 className={styles.cardTitle}>Account &amp; security</h3>
                        <p className={styles.cardText}>
                            Password, email, 2FA, recovery codes, and active sessions.
                        </p>
                        <div className={styles.cardActions}>
                            <Button href="/account/settings?tab=security" variant="flat" size="sm">
                                Open security
                            </Button>
                        </div>
                    </Panel>

                    <Panel title="Linked accounts">
                        <h3 className={styles.cardTitle}>Riot &amp; Discord</h3>
                        <p className={styles.cardText}>
                            Link your Riot account to unlock match stats once Riot approves
                            full API access for Extree Stats.
                        </p>
                        <div className={styles.cardActions}>
                            <Button href="/account/riot-profile" variant="flat" size="sm">
                                Riot profile
                            </Button>
                            <Button href="/account/settings" variant="ghost" size="sm">
                                Settings
                            </Button>
                        </div>
                    </Panel>

                    <Panel title="Saved players">
                        <h3 className={styles.cardTitle}>Nothing saved yet</h3>
                        <p className={styles.emptyNote}>
                            Players you save from public profiles will appear here — a quick
                            way to follow your own stats or your squad.
                        </p>
                    </Panel>
                </div>

                <Panel title="Recent activity">
                    <p className={styles.emptyNote}>
                        Once match data is live, this section will show your latest searches
                        and saved players. For now, use the public tracker to explore.
                    </p>
                    <div className={styles.cardActions} style={{ marginTop: 12 }}>
                        <Link to="/support">
                            <Button variant="ghost" size="sm">
                                Contact support
                            </Button>
                        </Link>
                    </div>
                </Panel>
            </div>
        </DashboardShell>
    );
}

FILE: src\pages\admin-audit\index.tsx
============================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { DashboardShell } from '../../widgets/dashboard-shell';
import { getAccount, getAdminAudit, type AccountUser, type AuditEntry } from '../../shared/api/statsAuth';
import { formatRelativeTime } from '../../shared/lib/formatRelativeTime';
import styles from './index.module.scss';

export function AdminAuditPage() {
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const [account, setAccount] = useState<AccountUser | null>(null);
    const [entries, setEntries] = useState<AuditEntry[] | null>(null);
    const [offset, setOffset] = useState(0);
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

    useEffect(() => {
        getAccount().then(({ user, banned }) => {
            if (banned || !user || !user.isAdmin) {
                navigate('/login');
                return;
            }
            setAccount(user);
        });
    }, [navigate]);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!account) return;
        setLoading(true);
        getAdminAudit(50, offset)
            .then(res => setEntries(res.entries))
            .catch(() => setEntries([]))
            .finally(() => setLoading(false));
    }, [account, offset]);

    const dashUser = account ? { email: account.email, isAdmin: account.isAdmin } : null;

    return (
        <DashboardShell
            user={dashUser}
            breadcrumb={{ to: '/admin/tickets', label: 'Back to tickets' }}
            title="Audit log"
            subtitle="Admin actions — bans, verifications, ticket replies."
            wide
        >
            <div className={styles.tableWrap}>
                {loading && <div className={styles.loading}>Loading…</div>}
                {!loading && entries && entries.length === 0 && <div className={styles.loading}>No entries yet.</div>}
                {!loading && entries && entries.length > 0 && (
                    <table className={styles.table}>
                        <thead>
                            <tr>
                                <th>When</th>
                                <th>Admin</th>
                                <th>Action</th>
                                <th>Target</th>
                                <th>IP</th>
                            </tr>
                        </thead>
                        <tbody>
                            {entries.map(e => (
                                <tr key={e.id}>
                                    <td>{formatRelativeTime(e.createdAt)}</td>
                                    <td>{e.adminEmail}</td>
                                    <td>
                                        <code>{e.action}</code>
                                    </td>
                                    <td>
                                        {e.targetType && (
                                            <span className={styles.target}>
                                                {e.targetType}:{e.targetId?.slice(0, 8)}
                                            </span>
                                        )}
                                    </td>
                                    <td>{e.ip || '—'}</td>
                                </tr>
                            ))}
                        </tbody>
                    </table>
                )}
            </div>
            <div className={styles.pagination}>
                <button disabled={offset === 0} onClick={() => setOffset(Math.max(0, offset - 50))}>
                    ← Prev
                </button>
                <span>offset {offset}</span>
                <button disabled={!entries || entries.length < 50} onClick={() => setOffset(offset + 50)}>
                    Next →
                </button>
            </div>
        </DashboardShell>
    );
}

FILE: src\pages\admin-canned-responses\index.tsx
============================================================
import { useEffect, useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { DashboardShell } from '../../widgets/dashboard-shell';
import { Panel } from '../../shared/ui/Panel';
import { Button } from '../../shared/ui/Button';
import { TextField } from '../../shared/ui/TextField';
import { TextArea } from '../../shared/ui/TextArea';
import { EmptyState } from '../../shared/ui/EmptyState';
import {
    getAccount,
    getCannedResponses,
    createCannedResponse,
    deleteCannedResponse,
    ApiError,
    type AccountUser,
    type CannedResponse,
} from '../../shared/api/statsAuth';
import styles from './index.module.scss';

// Отдельная страница для CRUD шаблонов. В форме ответа на тикет —
// только picker (CannedTemplatePicker), здесь — управление списком.
export function AdminCannedResponsesPage() {
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const [account, setAccount] = useState<AccountUser | null>(null);
    const [checking, setChecking] = useState(true);
    const [responses, setResponses] = useState<CannedResponse[] | null>(null);
    const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

    const [newTitle, setNewTitle] = useState('');
    const [newBody, setNewBody] = useState('');
    const [creating, setCreating] = useState(false);
    const [deletingId, setDeletingId] = useState<string | null>(null);

    useEffect(() => {
        getAccount().then(({ user, banned }) => {
            if (banned || !user || !user.isAdmin) {
                navigate('/login');
                return;
            }
            setAccount(user);
            setChecking(false);
        });
    }, [navigate]);

    function load() {
        getCannedResponses()
            .then(result => setResponses(result.responses))
            .catch(err => {
                setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : 'Failed to load templates');
                setResponses([]);
            });
    }

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!checking) load();
    }, [checking]);

    async function onCreate(e: FormEvent) {
        e.preventDefault();
        setError(null);
        setCreating(true);
        try {
            await createCannedResponse(newTitle.trim(), newBody.trim());
            setNewTitle('');
            setNewBody('');
            load();
        } catch (err) {
            setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : 'Failed to create template');
        } finally {
            setCreating(false);
        }
    }

    async function onDelete(id: string) {
        if (!confirm('Delete this template?')) return;
        setDeletingId(id);
        try {
            await deleteCannedResponse(id);
            load();
        } catch (err) {
            setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : 'Failed to delete template');
        } finally {
            setDeletingId(null);
        }
    }

    const dashUser = account ? { email: account.email, isAdmin: account.isAdmin } : null;

    return (
        <DashboardShell
            user={dashUser}
            breadcrumb={{ to: '/admin/tickets', label: 'Back to tickets' }}
            title="Canned responses"
            subtitle="Reusable reply templates available in the ticket reply form."
        >
            <Panel title="New template">
                <form className={styles.form} onSubmit={onCreate}>
                    <TextField
                        id="canned-title"
                        label="Title"
                        required
                        value={newTitle}
                        onChange={e => setNewTitle(e.target.value)}
                    />
                    <TextArea
                        id="canned-body"
                        label="Body"
                        required
                        value={newBody}
                        onChange={e => setNewBody(e.target.value)}
                    />
                    <div className={styles.formActions}>
                        <Button type="submit" variant="flat" disabled={creating || !newTitle.trim() || !newBody.trim()}>
                            {creating ? 'Creating…' : 'Create template'}
                        </Button>
                    </div>
                </form>
            </Panel>

            {error && <p className={styles.error}>{error}</p>}

            {responses === null && <p className={styles.loading}>Loading…</p>}
            {responses && responses.length === 0 && (
                <EmptyState title="No templates yet" description="Create your first reply template above." />
            )}
            {responses && responses.length > 0 && (
                <div className={styles.list}>
                    {responses.map(r => (
                        <Panel key={r.id} title={r.title}>
                            <div className={styles.bodyText}>{r.body}</div>
                            <div className={styles.itemActions}>
                                <Button
                                    variant="ghost"
                                    size="sm"
                                    onClick={() => onDelete(r.id)}
                                    disabled={deletingId === r.id}
                                >
                                    {deletingId === r.id ? 'Deleting…' : 'Delete'}
                                </Button>
                            </div>
                        </Panel>
                    ))}
                </div>
            )}
        </DashboardShell>
    );
}
