FILE: src\pages\admin-ticket\index.tsx
============================================================
import { useEffect, useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { StatusPill } from '../../shared/ui/StatusPill';
import { TextArea } from '../../shared/ui/TextArea';
import { Button } from '../../shared/ui/Button';
import { IconButtonGroup } from '../../shared/ui/IconButtonGroup';
import { EmptyState } from '../../shared/ui/EmptyState';
import { TicketThread } from '../../widgets/ticket-thread';
import { CannedTemplatePicker } from '../../widgets/canned-responses';
import { formatRelativeTime } from '../../shared/lib/formatRelativeTime';
import {
    getAdminTicket,
    replyToTicketAsAdmin,
    setTicketStatus,
    verifyUser,
    type AdminTicketDetail,
    type TicketReply,
    type TicketStatus,
} from '../../shared/api/statsAuth';
import styles from './index.module.scss';

const STATUS_OPTIONS = [
    { value: 'open', label: 'Open' },
    { value: 'in_progress', label: 'In progress' },
    { value: 'closed', label: 'Closed' },
] as const;

export function AdminTicketPage() {
    const { number } = useParams<{ number: string }>();
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const [ticket, setTicket] = useState<AdminTicketDetail | null>(null);
    const [replies, setReplies] = useState<TicketReply[]>([]);
    const [message, setMessage] = useState('');
    const [pending, setPending] = useState(false);
    const [isVerifying, setIsVerifying] = useState(false);
    const [loadError, setLoadError] = useState<string | null>(null);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!number) return;
        let cancelled = false;
        setLoadError(null);
        getAdminTicket(number)
            .then(res => {
                if (cancelled) return;
                setTicket(res.ticket);
                setReplies(res.replies);
            })
            .catch(() => {
                if (!cancelled) setLoadError('Failed to load ticket.');
            });
        return () => {
            cancelled = true;
        };
    }, [number]);

    async function onReply(e: FormEvent) {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (!number || !message.trim()) return;
        setPending(true);
        try {
            await replyToTicketAsAdmin(number, message);
            setMessage('');
            const res = await getAdminTicket(number);
            setTicket(res.ticket);
            setReplies(res.replies);
        } catch {
            alert('Failed to send reply');
        } finally {
            setPending(false);
        }
    }

    async function onStatusChange(newStatus: TicketStatus) {
        if (!number) return;
        try {
            await setTicketStatus(number, newStatus);
            setTicket(t => (t ? { ...t, status: newStatus } : null));
        } catch {
            alert('Failed to update status');
        }
    }

    async function handleVerify() {
        if (!ticket || !ticket.userId) return;
        setIsVerifying(true);
        try {
            const newStatus = !ticket.isUserVerified;
            await verifyUser(ticket.userId, newStatus);
            setTicket(t => (t ? { ...t, isUserVerified: newStatus } : null));
        } catch {
            alert('Failed to update verification status');
        } finally {
            setIsVerifying(false);
        }
    }

    if (loadError) {
        return (
            <div className={styles.center}>
                <p className={styles.errorText}>{loadError}</p>
            </div>
        );
    }

    if (!ticket) {
        return <div className={styles.center}>Loading…</div>;
    }

    const threadMessages = [
        { id: 'original', isSupport: false, authorLabel: 'User', message: ticket.message, time: ticket.time },
        ...replies.map(r => ({
            id: r.id,
            isSupport: r.author === 'admin',
            authorLabel: r.author === 'admin' ? 'Support (You)' : 'User',
            message: r.message,
            time: r.time,
        })),
    ];

    const statusIndex = STATUS_OPTIONS.findIndex(o => o.value === ticket.status);

    return (
        <div className={styles.page}>
            <header className={styles.header}>
                <button
                    type="button"
                    className={styles.backBtn}
                    onClick={() => navigate('/admin/tickets')}
                    aria-label="Back to tickets"
                >
                    ←
                </button>
                <div className={styles.headerMain}>
                    <div className={styles.headerTop}>
                        <span className={styles.number}>#{ticket.number}</span>
                        <StatusPill status={ticket.status} />
                    </div>
                    <h1 className={styles.title}>{ticket.subject}</h1>
                    <div className={styles.meta}>
                        <span className={styles.email}>{ticket.email}</span>
                        <span className={styles.sep}>·</span>
                        <span className={styles.category}>{ticket.category}</span>
                        <span className={styles.sep}>·</span>
                        <span className={styles.time}>opened {formatRelativeTime(ticket.time)}</span>
                    </div>
                </div>
                <div className={styles.headerActions}>
                    <IconButtonGroup
                        aria-label="Ticket actions"
                        items={[
                            {
                                key: 'open',
                                icon: <span className={styles.statusIcon}>○</span>,
                                label: 'Mark as Open',
                                onClick: () => onStatusChange('open'),
                                active: statusIndex === 0,
                                disabled: ticket.status === 'open',
                            },
                            {
                                key: 'in_progress',
                                icon: <span className={styles.statusIcon}>◐</span>,
                                label: 'Mark as In progress',
                                onClick: () => onStatusChange('in_progress'),
                                active: statusIndex === 1,
                                disabled: ticket.status === 'in_progress',
                            },
                            {
                                key: 'closed',
                                icon: <span className={styles.statusIcon}>●</span>,
                                label: 'Close ticket',
                                onClick: () => onStatusChange('closed'),
                                active: statusIndex === 2,
                                disabled: ticket.status === 'closed',
                            },
                        ]}
                    />
                    <IconButtonGroup
                        aria-label="User actions"
                        items={[
                            {
                                key: 'verify',
                                icon: <span className={styles.statusIcon}>{ticket.isUserVerified ? '✓' : '☐'}</span>,
                                label: ticket.isUserVerified ? 'Remove checkmark' : 'Give checkmark',
                                onClick: handleVerify,
                                variant: ticket.isUserVerified ? 'default' : 'accent',
                                active: ticket.isUserVerified,
                                disabled: isVerifying || !ticket.userId,
                            },
                        ]}
                    />
                </div>
            </header>

            <div className={styles.body}>
                <section className={styles.thread}>
                    <TicketThread messages={threadMessages} />
                </section>

                {ticket.status !== 'closed' ? (
                    <form className={styles.replyForm} onSubmit={onReply}>
                        <TextArea
                            id="reply"
                            label="Reply"
                            placeholder="Type your reply here…"
                            required
                            value={message}
                            onChange={e => setMessage(e.target.value)}
                        />
                        <div className={styles.replyActions}>
                            <CannedTemplatePicker
                                onPick={body => setMessage(prev => (prev ? `${prev}\n\n${body}` : body))}
                                disabled={pending}
                            />
                            <Button type="submit" variant="flat" disabled={pending || !message.trim()}>
                                {pending ? 'Sending…' : 'Send reply'}
                            </Button>
                        </div>
                    </form>
                ) : (
                    <EmptyState
                        title="This ticket is closed"
                        description="Reopen it to send a new reply."
                        action={
                            <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={() => onStatusChange('open')}>
                                Reopen ticket
                            </Button>
                        }
                    />
                )}
            </div>
        </div>
    );
}

                </svg>
            }

            description="Pick a ticket from the list on the left to view the conversation and reply."

FILE: src\pages\admin-users\index.tsx
============================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { DashboardShell } from '../../widgets/dashboard-shell';
import { Panel } from '../../shared/ui/Panel';
import { Avatar } from '../../shared/ui/Avatar';
import { IconButton } from '../../shared/ui/IconButton';
import { IconCheck, IconX, ShieldIcon, IconShieldOff } from '../../shared/ui/Icons';
import { useAuth } from '../../shared/auth/useAuth';
import { getAllUsers, banUser, verifyUser, makeAdmin, ApiError, type AdminUser } from '../../shared/api/statsAuth';
import { Skeleton } from '../../shared/ui/Skeleton';
import styles from './index.module.scss';

// SRP: только рендер страницы. Данные о текущем юзере — из useAuth
// (ProtectedRoute adminOnly гарантирует, что user !== null и isAdmin).
export function AdminUsersPage() {
    const { user: currentUser } = useAuth();
    const [users, setUsers] = useState<AdminUser[]>([]);
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
    const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const [pendingId, setPendingId] = useState<string | null>(null);

    useEffect(() => {
        getAllUsers()
            .then(res => setUsers(res.users))
            .catch(err => setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : 'Failed to load users'))
            .finally(() => setLoading(false));
    }, []);

    async function handleAction(id: string, action: () => Promise<unknown>, field: keyof AdminUser) {
        setPendingId(id);
        try {
            await action();
            setUsers(users.map(u => (u.id === id ? { ...u, [field]: !u[field] } : u)));
        } catch (err) {
            alert(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : `Failed to update ${field}`);
        } finally {
            setPendingId(null);
        }
    }

    if (loading) {
        return (
            <DashboardShell user={null} title="Users" wide>
                <Panel>
                    <div className={styles.skeletonList}>
                        {Array.from({ length: 6 }, (_, i) => (
                            <div key={i} className={styles.skeletonRow}>
                                <Skeleton width={36} height={36} radius="50%" />
                                <Skeleton width="40%" height={14} />
                                <Skeleton width={70} height={26} radius={999} />
                            </div>
                        ))}
                    </div>
                </Panel>
            </DashboardShell>
        );
    }

    return (
        <DashboardShell
            user={currentUser ? { email: currentUser.email, isAdmin: currentUser.isAdmin } : null}
            title="User Management"
            wide
        >
            {error && <div className={styles.errorBanner}>{error}</div>}

            <Panel noBodyPadding>
                <div className={styles.tableWrapper}>
                    <table className={styles.table}>
                        <thead>
                            <tr>
                                <th style={{ width: '40%' }}>User</th>
                                <th style={{ width: '15%' }}>Status</th>
                                <th style={{ width: '15%' }}>Role</th>
                                <th style={{ textAlign: 'right', paddingRight: '24px' }}>Actions</th>
                            </tr>
                        </thead>
                        <tbody>
                            {users.map(u => (
                                <tr key={u.id} className={u.isBanned ? styles.bannedRow : ''}>
                                    <td>
                                        <div className={styles.userCell}>
                                            <Avatar label={u.nickname || u.name || u.email} />
                                            <div>
                                                <div className={styles.userName}>
                                                    {u.nickname || u.name || 'Unknown'}
                                                    {u.isVerified && (
                                                        <span className={styles.blueTick} title="Verified">
                                                            <svg
                                                                width="14"
                                                                height="14"
                                                                viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                                                                fill="currentColor"
                                                            >
                                                                <path d="M9 16.17L4.83 12l-1.42 1.41L9 19 21 7l-1.41-1.41z" />
                                                            </svg>
                                                        </span>
                                                    )}
                                                </div>
                                                <div className={styles.userEmail}>{u.email}</div>
                                            </div>
                                        </div>
                                    </td>
                                    <td>
                                        <span
                                            className={`${styles.statusBadge} ${u.isBanned ? styles.statusBanned : styles.statusActive}`}
                                        >
                                            <span className={styles.dot} />
                                            {u.isBanned ? 'Banned' : 'Active'}
                                        </span>
                                    </td>
                                    <td>
                                        {u.isAdmin ? (
                                            <span className={styles.adminBadge}>
                                                <ShieldIcon /> Admin
                                            </span>
                                        ) : (
                                            <span className={styles.roleUser}>User</span>
                                        )}
                                    </td>
                                    <td>
                                        <div className={styles.actions}>
                                            <IconButton
                                                variant={u.isVerified ? 'active' : 'default'}
                                                onClick={() =>
                                                    handleAction(
                                                        u.id,
                                                        () => verifyUser(u.id, !u.isVerified),
                                                        'isVerified'
                                                    )
                                                }
                                                disabled={pendingId === u.id}
                                                title={u.isVerified ? 'Remove Verification' : 'Verify User'}
                                            >
                                                {u.isVerified ? <IconX /> : <IconCheck />}
                                            </IconButton>

                                            <IconButton
                                                variant={u.isAdmin ? 'active' : 'default'}
                                                onClick={() =>
                                                    handleAction(u.id, () => makeAdmin(u.id, !u.isAdmin), 'isAdmin')
                                                }
                                                disabled={pendingId === u.id}
                                                title={u.isAdmin ? 'Remove Admin' : 'Make Admin'}
                                            >
                                                {u.isAdmin ? <IconShieldOff /> : <ShieldIcon />}
                                            </IconButton>

                                            <IconButton
                                                variant={u.isBanned ? 'default' : 'danger'}
                                                onClick={() =>
                                                    handleAction(u.id, () => banUser(u.id, !u.isBanned), 'isBanned')
                                                }
                                                disabled={pendingId === u.id}
                                                title={u.isBanned ? 'Unban User' : 'Ban User'}
                                            >
                                                {u.isBanned ? <IconCheck /> : <IconX />}
                                            </IconButton>
                                        </div>
                                    </td>
                                </tr>
                            ))}
                        </tbody>
                    </table>
                </div>
            </Panel>
        </DashboardShell>
    );
}

FILE: src\pages\cookies\index.module.scss
============================================================
@use '../../shared/styles/variables' as v;

.page {
    position: relative;
    min-height: 100vh;
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: center;
}

.main {
    width: 100%;

import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { SiteHeader } from '../../widgets/site-header';
import { Button } from '../../shared/ui/Button';
import { useConsent } from '../../shared/consent/useConsent';
import styles from './index.module.scss';

// Вынесено из общего абзаца про куки в pages/privacy в отдельную
// страницу — тот же контент, что уже был в privacy ("How it's stored"),
// но как самостоятельный, предметный Cookie Policy документ: именно так
// его ищут (/cookies), а не как подраздел в середине privacy policy.
//
// Три необходимых куки (сессия, CSRF-токен, Turnstile) — строго
// технические, ни одна не требует согласия по GDPR/ePrivacy (strictly
// necessary исключены из требования consent). Но мы также используем два
// non-essential сторонних сервиса (Sentry, Google Fonts) — вот под них и
// нужен баннер (widgets/cookie-consent) и управление категориями ниже.
export function CookiesPage() {
    const { consent, reopen } = useConsent();

    return (
        <div className={styles.page}>
            <SiteHeader
                rightSlot={
                    <>
                        <Link to="/terms">Terms of Use</Link>
                        <span aria-hidden="true"> · </span>
                        <Link to="/privacy">Privacy Policy</Link>
                    </>
                }
            />
            <main className={styles.main}>
                <article className={styles.article}>
                    <p className={styles.updated}>Last updated: October 25, 2026</p>
                    <h1>Cookie Policy</h1>
                    <p>
                        This page lists every cookie and third-party service Extree Stats (stats.extree.tech) uses, what
                        each is for, and how long it lasts.
                    </p>

                    <h2>Necessary — always on</h2>
                    <p>
                        These keep the site working and secure. They&apos;re strictly necessary under GDPR/ePrivacy, so
                        they don&apos;t require consent and can&apos;t be turned off.
                    </p>
                    <div className={styles.table}>
                        <div className={`${styles.row} ${styles.rowHead}`}>
                            <span>Name</span>
                            <span>Purpose</span>
                            <span>Duration</span>
                        </div>
                        <div className={styles.row}>
                            <code>extree_stats_session</code>
                            <span>
                                Keeps you signed in. Holds an encrypted, <code>httpOnly</code> token identifying your
                                account — it can&apos;t be read or modified from your browser.
                            </span>
                            <span>7 days, or until you sign out</span>
                        </div>
                        <div className={styles.row}>
                            <code>csrf</code>
                            <span>Prevents cross-site request forgery on forms and account actions.</span>
                            <span>2 hours</span>
                        </div>
                        <div className={styles.row}>
                            <code>extree_stats_oauth_state</code>
                            <span>Prevents cross-site request forgery during Riot Sign-On (RSO) redirects.</span>
                            <span>10 minutes, or until the sign-in redirect completes</span>
                        </div>
                        <div className={styles.row}>
                            <code>cf_*</code>
                            <span>
                                Set by Cloudflare Turnstile, the bot-check widget on the sign-in and registration forms.
                                We don&apos;t control these directly — see{' '}
                                <a href="https://www.cloudflare.com/cookie-policy/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">
                                    Cloudflare&apos;s cookie policy
                                </a>
                                .
                            </span>
                            <span>Varies (Cloudflare-managed)</span>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <h2>Optional — only with your consent</h2>
                    <p>These aren&apos;t required for the site to work. We only use them if you opt in.</p>
                    <div className={styles.table}>
                        <div className={`${styles.row} ${styles.rowHead}`}>
                            <span>Service</span>
                            <span>Purpose</span>
                            <span>Status</span>
                        </div>
                        <div className={styles.row}>
                            <code>Sentry</code>
                            <span>
                                Error monitoring — reports crashes and bugs so we can fix them. Sends your browser info
                                and IP to Sentry only when something breaks.
                            </span>
                            <span>{consent?.analytics ? 'Enabled' : 'Disabled'}</span>
                        </div>
                        <div className={styles.row}>
                            <code>Google Fonts</code>
                            <span>
                                Loads the Inter typeface. Declining just falls back to your system font — nothing
                                breaks.
                            </span>
                            <span>{consent?.preferences ? 'Enabled' : 'Disabled'}</span>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <h2>Manage your preferences</h2>
                    <p>
                        Change your mind any time — this reopens the same choice you got on your first visit (Accept all
                        / Necessary only / Customize).
                    </p>
                    <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={reopen}>
                        Manage cookie preferences
                    </Button>

                    <h2>More on how we handle data</h2>
                    <p>
                        This page covers cookies and third-party scripts specifically. For what account data we store
                        and why, see our <Link to="/privacy">Privacy Policy</Link>.
                    </p>

                    <h2>Changes</h2>
                    <p>
                        This policy may change if we add new cookies or services in the future. The date above reflects
                        the latest update.
                    </p>

                    <h2>Contact</h2>
                    <p>
                        Questions about cookies: <a href="mailto:support@extree.tech">support@extree.tech</a>.
                    </p>
                </article>
            </main>
            <footer className={styles.footer}>part of extree.tech</footer>
        </div>
    );
}

FILE: src\pages\forgot-password\index.tsx
============================================================
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { SplitAuthLayout } from '../../widgets/split-auth';
import { TextField } from '../../shared/ui/TextField';
import { Button } from '../../shared/ui/Button';
import { forgotPassword, ApiError } from '../../shared/api/statsAuth';
import styles from './index.module.scss';

export function ForgotPasswordPage() {
    const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
    const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const [pending, setPending] = useState(false);
    const [sent, setSent] = useState(false);

    async function onSubmit(e: FormEvent) {
        e.preventDefault();
        setError(null);
        setPending(true);
        try {
            await forgotPassword(email);
            // Один и тот же ответ независимо от того, существует ли
            // аккаунт — сервер нарочно не сообщает (см. server.js), и
            // UI показывает один и тот же текст, а не угадывает.
            setSent(true);
        } catch (err) {
            setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : 'Something went wrong — please try again.');
        } finally {
            setPending(false);
        }
    }

    return (
        <SplitAuthLayout
            badge="Forgot your password?"
            heading={
                <>
                    Reset your
                    <br />
                    <span className="hl">password.</span>
                </>
            }
            lead="Enter the email on your account and we'll send you a link to set a new password."
            cardTitle="Reset password"
            error={error}
            footer={
                <>
                    Remembered it? <Link to="/login">Log in</Link>
                </>
            }
        >
            {sent ? (
                <p className={styles.sent}>
                    If <strong>{email}</strong> is registered with us, you&apos;ll get a reset link shortly. Check your
                    inbox (and spam folder).
                </p>
            ) : (
                <form className={styles.form} onSubmit={onSubmit}>
                    <TextField
                        id="email"
                        label="Email"
                        type="email"
                        autoComplete="email"
                        required
                        value={email}
                        onChange={e => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                    />
                    <Button type="submit" variant="flat" fullWidth disabled={pending}>
                        {pending ? 'Sending…' : 'Send reset link'}
                    </Button>
                </form>
            )}
        </SplitAuthLayout>
    );
}

FILE: src\pages\game-hub\index.tsx
============================================================
import { Link, useParams, Navigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { SiteHeader } from '../../widgets/site-header';
import { SearchBar } from '../../shared/ui/SearchBar';
import { getGame } from '../../shared/config/games';
import styles from './index.module.scss';

// Публичная страница конкретной игры. Сюда ведут карточки с главной.
// Пока для VALORANT показывает поиск + плейсхолдер (жадём Riot API).
// Для coming_soon игр — не роутится (см. HomePage), но если попали
// напрямую по URL — показываем "not available yet".
export function GameHubPage() {
    const { slug } = useParams<{ slug: string }>();
    const game = slug ? getGame(slug) : undefined;

    if (!game) return <Navigate to="/" replace />;

    const isPending = game.status === 'pending_api';
    const isLive = game.status === 'ready';

    return (
        <div className={styles.page}>
            <SiteHeader showSearch />

            <section className={styles.hero}>
                <Link to="/" className={styles.breadcrumb}>← All games</Link>
                <h1 className={styles.title}>{game.name}</h1>
                <div className={styles.statusLine}>
                    <span
                        className={styles.dot}
                        style={{
                            background: isLive ? '#34d399' : isPending ? '#fbbf24' : '#71717a',
                            boxShadow: `0 0 8px ${
                                isLive ? '#34d399' : isPending ? '#fbbf24' : '#71717a'
                            }`,
                        }}
                    />
                    {isLive
                        ? 'Live — full stats available'
                        : isPending
                        ? 'Preview — Riot API access pending'
                        : 'Coming soon'}
                </div>
                <p className={styles.infoText} style={{ maxWidth: 560 }}>
                    {game.description}
                </p>

                {(isLive || isPending) && (
                    <div className={styles.searchRow}>
                        <SearchBar
                            gameSlug={game.slug}
                            placeholder={
                                game.slug === 'valorant'
                                    ? 'Search Riot ID — Name#TAG'
                                    : `Search ${game.name} player`
                            }
                        />
                    </div>
                )}

                <div className={styles.infoGrid}>
                    <div className={styles.infoCard}>
                        <h3 className={styles.infoTitle}>Match history</h3>
                        <p className={styles.infoText}>
                            {isLive
                                ? 'Full round-by-round breakdown for every match.'
                                : 'Unlocks automatically once API access is granted.'}
                        </p>
                    </div>
                    <div className={styles.infoCard}>
                        <h3 className={styles.infoTitle}>Rank tracking</h3>
                        <p className={styles.infoText}>
                            {isLive
                                ? 'Current rank, RR progression, peak rank.'
                                : 'Official ranks only — never a custom ELO.'}
                        </p>
                    </div>
                    <div className={styles.infoCard}>
                        <h3 className={styles.infoTitle}>Advanced stats</h3>
                        <p className={styles.infoText}>
                            {isLive
                                ? 'K/D, headshot %, win rate by map and agent.'
                                : 'Same visual language as the live dashboard.'}
                        </p>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </section>

            <section className={styles.section}>
                <h2 className={styles.sectionTitle}>Leaderboards</h2>
                <div className={styles.placeholder}>
                    {isLive
                        ? 'Leaderboards are being computed — check back soon.'
                        : isPending
                        ? 'Leaderboards unlock with full API access. Meanwhile, use the search above to preview a profile.'
                        : 'This game will be added in a future update.'}
                </div>
            </section>
        </div>
    );
}

import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { GAMES } from '../../shared/config/games';
import styles from './index.module.scss';

const TOP_STATS = [
    { name: 'Fortnite', count: '2.28M', change: '+6.0%', up: true },
    { name: 'Counter-Strike 2', count: '1.40M', change: '-3.0%', up: false },
    { name: 'Dota 2', count: '1.10M', change: '+14.0%', up: true },
    { name: 'PUBG', count: '816K', change: '+3.0%', up: true },
];

const GAME_COVERS: Record<string, string> = {

FILE: src\pages\login\index.tsx
============================================================
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { SplitAuthLayout } from '../../widgets/split-auth';
import { TextField } from '../../shared/ui/TextField';
import { Button } from '../../shared/ui/Button';
import { Checkbox } from '../../shared/ui/Checkbox';
import { Turnstile } from '../../shared/ui/Turnstile';
import { TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY } from '../../shared/config';
import { login, verify2fa, ApiError } from '../../shared/api/statsAuth';
import { useAuth } from '../../shared/auth/useAuth';
import styles from './index.module.scss';

export function LoginPage() {
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const { refresh } = useAuth();
    const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
    const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
    const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const [pending, setPending] = useState(false);
    const [turnstileToken, setTurnstileToken] = useState('');
    // Токен Turnstile одноразовый — бэкенд списывает его при каждой
    // попытке входа, даже неудачной (см. stats-backend/server.js). Смена
    // key пересоздаёт виджет и даёт свежий токен вместо попытки
    // переиспользовать уже потраченный.
    const [turnstileKey, setTurnstileKey] = useState(0);
    const [rememberMe, setRememberMe] = useState(true);
    const [requires2fa, setRequires2fa] = useState(false);
    const [totpCode, setTotpCode] = useState('');

    async function onSubmit(e: FormEvent) {
        e.preventDefault();
        setError(null);
        setPending(true);
        try {
            if (requires2fa) {
                await verify2fa(email, totpCode, rememberMe);
                await refresh();
                navigate('/account');
                return;
            }
            await login(email, password, turnstileToken, rememberMe);
            await refresh();
            navigate('/account');
        } catch (err) {
            if (err instanceof ApiError && err.code === 'email_not_verified') {
                navigate(`/verify?email=${encodeURIComponent(email)}`);
                return;
            }
            if (err instanceof ApiError && err.code === 'unknown_location') {
                navigate(`/verify-location?email=${encodeURIComponent(email)}`);
                return;
            }
            if (err instanceof ApiError && err.code === 'requires_2fa') {
                setRequires2fa(true);
                return;
            }
            if (err instanceof ApiError && err.code === 'banned') {
                setError(err.message);
                return;
            }
            setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : 'Something went wrong — please try again.');
            setTurnstileToken('');
            setTurnstileKey(k => k + 1);
        } finally {
            setPending(false);
        }
    }

    return (
        <SplitAuthLayout
            badge="Welcome back"
            heading={
                <>
                    Good to
                    <br />
                    <span className="hl">see you again.</span>
                </>
            }
            lead="Sign in to check your win rates, K/D trends, and rank progress — right where you left off."
            cardTitle="Sign in"
            error={error}
            footer={
                <>
                    Don&apos;t have an account? <Link to="/register">Create one</Link>
                </>
            }
        >
            <form className={styles.form} onSubmit={onSubmit}>
                <TextField
                    id="email"
                    label="Email"
                    type="email"
                    autoComplete="email"
                    required
                    value={email}
                    onChange={e => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                />
                <TextField
                    id="password"
                    label="Password"
                    type="password"
                    autoComplete="current-password"
                    required
                    value={password}
                    onChange={e => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                />
                <div className={styles.row}>
                    <Checkbox
                        id="remember-me"
                        label="Remember me"
                        checked={rememberMe}
                        onChange={e => setRememberMe(e.target.checked)}
                    />
                    <Link to="/forgot-password" className={styles.forgot}>
                        Forgot password?
                    </Link>
                </div>
                <Turnstile
                    key={turnstileKey}
                    siteKey={TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY}
                    onVerify={setTurnstileToken}
                    onExpire={() => setTurnstileToken('')}
                />
                {requires2fa ? (
                    <>
                        <TextField
                            id="totp-code"
                            label="2FA code"
                            type="text"
                            inputMode="numeric"
                            autoComplete="one-time-code"
                            required
                            value={totpCode}
                            onChange={e => setTotpCode(e.target.value)}
                        />
                        <Button type="submit" variant="flat" fullWidth disabled={pending || totpCode.length < 6}>
                            {pending ? 'Verifying…' : 'Verify 2FA'}
                        </Button>
                    </>
                ) : (
                    <>
                        <Button
                            type="submit"
                            variant="flat"
                            fullWidth
                            disabled={pending || (requires2fa ? totpCode.length < 6 : !turnstileToken)}
                        >
                            {pending ? 'Signing in…' : 'Sign in'}
                        </Button>
                    </>
                )}
            </form>
        </SplitAuthLayout>
    );
}

FILE: src\pages\player-profile\index.tsx

import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { DashboardShell } from '../../widgets/dashboard-shell';
import { Panel } from '../../shared/ui/Panel';
import { TextField } from '../../shared/ui/TextField';
import { Button } from '../../shared/ui/Button';
import { Skeleton } from '../../shared/ui/Skeleton';
import { ChartIcon, TargetIcon, ShieldIcon, LockIcon } from '../../shared/ui/Icons';
import { useAuth } from '../../shared/auth/useAuth';
import { lookupRiotId, ApiError } from '../../shared/api/statsAuth';
import styles from './index.module.scss';

const RIOT_DISCLAIMER =
    "Extree Stats isn't endorsed by Riot Games and doesn't reflect the views or opinions of Riot Games or anyone officially involved in producing or managing VALORANT. VALORANT and Riot Games are trademarks or registered trademarks of Riot Games, Inc.";

const LOCKED_STATS = [
    { label: 'K/D Ratio', icon: ChartIcon },
    { label: 'Win Rate', icon: TargetIcon },
    { label: 'Headshot %', icon: TargetIcon },
    { label: 'Current Rank', icon: ShieldIcon },
];

// Настоящая реализация "профиля игрока" (раньше кнопка "View Riot
// Profile" в Linked Accounts вела на /riot-lookup, который не
// подключён в App.tsx — см. комментарий там про RSO/VALORANT API
// Pending Review — мёртвая ссылка). Данные те же, что уже честно
// показывает /api/riot/lookup (account-v1: подтверждение, что аккаунт
// существует), просто оформлены как собственная страница профиля
// внутри панели управления, а не как отдельный публичный инструмент
// поиска. Матч-статистику не выдумываем — секция с ней заблокирована
// и так и подписана, пока Riot не одобрит полный доступ к API.
export function PlayerProfilePage() {
    const { user } = useAuth();
    const [riotId, setRiotId] = useState('');
    const [result, setResult] = useState<{ gameName: string; tagLine: string } | null>(null);
    const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const [pending, setPending] = useState(false);

    async function onSubmit(e: FormEvent) {
        e.preventDefault();
        setError(null);
        setPending(true);
        try {
            const found = await lookupRiotId(riotId);
            setResult({ gameName: found.gameName, tagLine: found.tagLine });
        } catch (err) {
            setResult(null);
            setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : 'Something went wrong — please try again.');
        } finally {
            setPending(false);
        }
    }

    const dashUser = user ? { email: user.email, isAdmin: user.isAdmin } : null;

    return (
        <DashboardShell
            user={dashUser}
            breadcrumb={{ to: '/account/settings', label: 'Back to settings' }}
            title="Player Profile"
            subtitle="Confirm a Riot ID through Riot's API — full match stats unlock once Riot approves full access."
        >
            {!user ? (
                <Panel>
                    <div className={styles.skeletonRow}>
                        <Skeleton width={64} height={64} radius="50%" />
                        <Skeleton width={160} height={20} />
                    </div>
                </Panel>
            ) : (
                <>
                    <Panel
                        title="Look up a Riot ID"
                        subtitle="Enter a Riot ID to confirm it exists via Riot's account API."
                    >
                        <form className={styles.form} onSubmit={onSubmit}>
                            <TextField
                                id="riotId"
                                label="Riot ID"
                                type="text"
                                placeholder="Name#Tag"
                                required
                                value={riotId}
                                onChange={e => setRiotId(e.target.value)}
                            />
                            <Button type="submit" variant="flat" disabled={pending}>
                                {pending ? 'Looking up…' : 'Look up'}
                            </Button>
                            {error && (
                                <p className={styles.formError} role="alert">
                                    {error}
                                </p>
                            )}
                        </form>
                    </Panel>

                    {result && (
                        <Panel>
                            <div className={styles.profileHeader}>
                                <div className={styles.profileRing}>{result.gameName.slice(0, 2).toUpperCase()}</div>
                                <div className={styles.profileHeaderText}>
                                    <div className={styles.profileName}>
                                        {result.gameName}
                                        <span className={styles.profileTag}>#{result.tagLine}</span>
                                    </div>
                                    <span className={styles.confirmedBadge}>Account confirmed via Riot API</span>
                                </div>
                            </div>

                            <div className={styles.lockedGrid}>
                                {LOCKED_STATS.map(({ label, icon: Icon }) => (
                                    <div key={label} className={styles.lockedTile}>
                                        <div className={styles.lockedTileHead}>
                                            <Icon />
                                            <LockIcon />
                                        </div>
                                        <div className={styles.lockedValue}>—</div>
                                        <div className={styles.lockedLabel}>{label}</div>
                                    </div>
                                ))}
                            </div>
                            <p className={styles.lockedNote}>
                                Match history and detailed stats unlock automatically once Riot approves full API access
                                for Extree Stats — nothing further to do on your end.
                            </p>
                        </Panel>
                    )}

                    <p className={styles.disclaimer}>{RIOT_DISCLAIMER}</p>
                </>
            )}
        </DashboardShell>
    );
}

FILE: src\pages\privacy\index.tsx
============================================================
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { SiteHeader } from '../../widgets/site-header';
import styles from './index.module.scss';

// Приват-полиси — отдельный обязательный пункт чеклиста Riot Developer
// Portal наравне с Terms of Use (developer.riotgames.com/policies/
// general требует оба документа у любого продукта на их API, особенно
// с OAuth-логином). Текст написан по факту того, что реально делает
// stats-backend/ — не шаблон, и обновлён вместе с добавлением
// email+пароль-аккаунтов (см. stats-backend/users.js/db.js): теперь это
// больше не "нет базы данных", честность важнее, чем звучать проще.
export function PrivacyPage() {
    return (
        <div className={styles.page}>
            <SiteHeader
                rightSlot={
                    <>
                        <Link to="/terms">Terms of Use</Link>
                        <span aria-hidden="true"> · </span>
                        <Link to="/cookies">Cookie Policy</Link>
                    </>
                }
            />
            <main className={styles.main}>
                <article className={styles.article}>
                    <p className={styles.updated}>Last updated: October 20, 2026</p>
                    <h1>Privacy Policy</h1>
                    <p>
                        This page explains what Extree Stats (stats.extree.tech) collects, whether you sign in with
                        email or with your Riot account, why, and how to have it removed.
                    </p>

                    <h2>Email + password accounts</h2>
                    <p>
                        If you create an account, we store your email address and a hashed password (never the password
                        itself — it&apos;s hashed with scrypt and can&apos;t be reversed) in our database. During
                        sign-up, we email a 6-digit code to confirm the address is really yours; that code is deleted
                        once used or after 10 minutes, whichever comes first.
                    </p>
                    <p>
                        Verification emails are sent from a mailbox dedicated to Extree Stats, separate from the rest of
                        the extree.tech infrastructure.
                    </p>

                    <h2>Signing in with Riot (RSO)</h2>
                    <p>
                        When you sign in through Riot Sign-On, Riot sends us your account&apos;s unique identifier (the{' '}
                        <code>sub</code> claim from Riot&apos;s OpenID Connect <code>/userinfo</code> endpoint) so we
                        know which Riot account is signed in. We never see or store your Riot password — authentication
                        happens entirely on Riot&apos;s own servers. Linking a Riot account to your Extree Stats account
                        is planned but not available yet — per Riot&apos;s own policies, linking it will require
                        accepting a separate, explicit notice that doing so makes your VALORANT stats visible to other
                        Extree Stats users who look you up. We won&apos;t show match data for any Riot account that
                        hasn&apos;t opted in that way itself.
                    </p>

                    <h2>How it&apos;s stored</h2>
                    <p>
                        Account data (email, hashed password, and — once linked — your Riot identifier) lives in our
                        database. Your signed-in session itself is a single encrypted, <code>httpOnly</code> cookie (
                        <code>extree_stats_session</code>), not a server-side session store — it&apos;s encrypted with
                        AES-256-GCM, can&apos;t be read or modified from your browser, and expires automatically after 7
                        days or immediately when you sign out.
                    </p>
                    <p>
                        A second, short-lived cookie (<code>extree_stats_oauth_state</code>) exists only during the Riot
                        sign-in redirect itself, to prevent cross-site request forgery — it&apos;s deleted within
                        minutes either way. A third (<code>csrf</code>) protects form submissions the same way, for up
                        to 2 hours.
                    </p>
                    <p>
                        None of these cookies are used for analytics, advertising, or tracking across other sites. We do
                        use two optional, non-essential services (error monitoring and web fonts) — both off by default
                        until you consent. See our <Link to="/cookies">Cookie Policy</Link> for the full list, exact
                        durations, and how to change your choice.
                    </p>

                    <h2>Sign-in security (IP address &amp; location)</h2>
                    <p>
                        When you sign in, we look up the approximate country (and sometimes city) your IP address
                        belongs to, using a local, offline database on our own server — no third-party geolocation
                        service is called, and your IP address is never sent anywhere outside our infrastructure. We
                        keep the country tied to your account so we can recognize future sign-ins from the same place.
                    </p>
                    <p>
                        Signing in from a country we haven&apos;t seen for your account before requires confirming a
                        one-time code we email you, the same way as during sign-up. After every successful sign-in, we
                        also email you a notification with the approximate time, location, and IP address, so you can
                        tell if it wasn&apos;t you.
                    </p>
                    <p>
                        We don&apos;t keep a full history of every IP address you&apos;ve ever signed in from — only the
                        countries we currently recognize for your account.
                    </p>

                    <h2>VALORANT match data</h2>
                    <p>
                        Once Riot account linking and match history are live, that data will be requested from the
                        official VALORANT API server-side, on demand, for your account only — not in bulk, not on a
                        schedule, and not cached beyond what&apos;s needed to render the page you asked for.
                    </p>

                    <h2>Who we share data with</h2>
                    <p>
                        We don&apos;t sell or share your data with third parties. The outside parties involved are Riot
                        Games (whose API and Sign-On service this product is built on) and our email provider (used only
                        to deliver verification codes to the address you give us). Extree Stats is a separate product
                        from the Extree Discord bot and its dashboard — the two don&apos;t share accounts, sessions, or
                        data.
                    </p>

                    <h2>Deleting your data</h2>
                    <p>
                        Signing out clears your session cookie immediately. To delete your account and the data tied to
                        it, email us — we don&apos;t have self-serve deletion yet, but we&apos;ll remove it by hand on
                        request.
                    </p>

                    <h2>Children</h2>
                    <p>
                        Extree Stats isn&apos;t directed at children. If you link a Riot account, that&apos;s also
                        subject to Riot&apos;s own age requirements for account creation.
                    </p>

                    <h2>Changes</h2>
                    <p>
                        This policy may change as the product develops, in particular once Riot account linking and
                        match-data features go live. The date above reflects the latest update.
                    </p>

                    <h2>Contact</h2>
                    <p>
                        Questions about your data, or to request deletion:{' '}
                        <a href="mailto:support@extree.tech">support@extree.tech</a>.
                    </p>
                </article>
            </main>
            <footer className={styles.footer}>part of extree.tech</footer>
        </div>
    );
}

FILE: src\pages\public-player\index.tsx
============================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useParams, Navigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { SiteHeader } from '../../widgets/site-header';
import { getGame } from '../../shared/config/games';
import { lookupRiotId, ApiError } from '../../shared/api/statsAuth';
import styles from './index.module.scss';

type Tab = 'overview' | 'matches' | 'agents';

interface PlayerState {
    status: 'loading' | 'found' | 'not_found' | 'error';
    gameName?: string;
    tagLine?: string;
    error?: string;
}

export function PublicPlayerPage() {
    const { game: gameSlug, riotId } = useParams<{ game: string; riotId: string }>();
    const game = gameSlug ? getGame(gameSlug) : undefined;
    const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>('overview');
    const [player, setPlayer] = useState<PlayerState>({ status: 'loading' });

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!game || !riotId) return;
        if (game.slug !== 'valorant') {
            setPlayer({ status: 'not_found', error: 'This game is not available yet.' });
            return;
        }

        let cancelled = false;
        setPlayer({ status: 'loading' });

        lookupRiotId(decodeURIComponent(riotId))
            .then(res => {
                if (cancelled) return;
                setPlayer({ status: 'found', gameName: res.gameName, tagLine: res.tagLine });
            })
            .catch((err: unknown) => {
                if (cancelled) return;
                setPlayer({
                    status: 'error',
                    error: err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : 'Could not look up this player.',
                });
            });

        return () => { cancelled = true; };
    }, [game, riotId]);

    if (!game || !riotId) return <Navigate to="/" replace />;

    const displayName = player.gameName ?? decodeURIComponent(riotId).split('#')[0];
    const displayTag = player.tagLine ?? decodeURIComponent(riotId).split('#')[1] ?? '';
    const initial = displayName.slice(0, 1).toUpperCase();

    // Заглушки статов — в будущем заменятся реальными данными API
    const statPlaceholders = [
        { label: 'K/D Ratio', value: '—' },
        { label: 'Win Rate', value: '—' },
        { label: 'Headshot %', value: '—' },
        { label: 'Current Rank', value: '—' },
        { label: 'Matches', value: '—' },
        { label: 'Hours', value: '—' },
    ];

    return (
        <div className={styles.page}>
            <SiteHeader showSearch />

            <div className={styles.container}>
                <Link to={`/${game.slug}`} className={styles.breadcrumb}>
                    ← {game.name}
                </Link>

                <div className={styles.header}>
                    <div className={styles.avatar}>{initial}</div>
                    <div className={styles.headerText}>
                        <h1 className={styles.name}>
                            {displayName}
                            {displayTag && <span className={styles.tag}>#{displayTag}</span>}
                        </h1>
                        <span className={styles.gameBadge}>
                            {game.shortName}
                            {player.status === 'found' && ' · Confirmed'}
                        </span>
                    </div>
                </div>

                {player.status === 'error' && <div className={styles.error}>{player.error}</div>}
                {player.status === 'loading' && <div className={styles.loading}>Looking up player…</div>}

                {player.status === 'found' && (
                    <>
                        <div className={styles.tabs}>
                            {(['overview', 'matches', 'agents'] as Tab[]).map(t => (
                                <button
                                    key={t}
                                    className={`${styles.tab} ${tab === t ? styles.tabActive : ''}`}
                                    onClick={() => setTab(t)}
                                >
                                    {t === 'overview' ? 'Overview' : t === 'matches' ? 'Matches' : 'Agents'}
                                </button>
                            ))}
                        </div>

                        <div className={styles.statsGrid}>
                            {statPlaceholders.map(s => (
                                <div key={s.label} className={styles.statCard}>
                                    <div className={styles.statLabel}>{s.label}</div>
                                    <div className={`${styles.statValue} ${s.value === '—' ? styles.statValueMuted : ''}`}>
                                        {s.value}
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            ))}
                        </div>

                        <div className={styles.lockedBanner}>
                            <h3 className={styles.lockedTitle}>Stats are locked</h3>
                            <p className={styles.lockedText}>
                                Match history and detailed stats unlock automatically once Riot
                                approves full API access for Extree Stats. The account is already
                                confirmed — nothing to do on your end.
                            </p>
                        </div>
                    </>
                )}

                {player.status === 'not_found' && (
                    <div className={styles.lockedBanner}>
                        <h3 className={styles.lockedTitle}>Player not found</h3>
                        <p className={styles.lockedText}>{player.error ?? 'Check the nickname and try again.'}</p>
                    </div>
                )}
            </div>
        </div>
    );
}

FILE: src\pages\register\index.tsx
============================================================
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { SplitAuthLayout } from '../../widgets/split-auth';
import { TextField } from '../../shared/ui/TextField';
import { Button } from '../../shared/ui/Button';
import { PasswordRequirements } from '../../shared/ui/PasswordRequirements';
import { isPasswordValid } from '../../shared/lib/passwordRules';
import { register, ApiError } from '../../shared/api/statsAuth';
import styles from './index.module.scss';

const NICKNAME_RE = /^[a-zA-Z0-9_]{3,24}$/;

export function RegisterPage() {
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const [name, setName] = useState('');
    const [nickname, setNickname] = useState('');
    const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
    const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
    const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState('');
    const [agreed, setAgreed] = useState(false);
    const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const [pending, setPending] = useState(false);

    const passwordsMatch = confirmPassword.length === 0 || confirmPassword === password;
    const canSubmit =
        name.trim().length > 0 &&
        NICKNAME_RE.test(nickname) &&
        isPasswordValid(password) &&
        confirmPassword === password &&
        agreed;

    async function onSubmit(e: FormEvent) {
        e.preventDefault();
        setError(null);
        setPending(true);
        try {
            await register(email, password, name.trim(), nickname);
            navigate(`/verify?email=${encodeURIComponent(email)}`);
        } catch (err) {
            setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : 'Something went wrong — please try again.');
        } finally {
            setPending(false);
        }
    }

    return (
        <SplitAuthLayout
            badge="Free account — 30 seconds"
            heading={
                <>
                    Track your game.
                    <br />
                    <span className="hl">Match by match.</span>
                </>
            }
            lead="Create a free account to save your lookups, track trends over time, and unlock your activity feed."
            cardTitle="Create account"
            error={error}
            footer={
                <>
                    Already have an account? <Link to="/login">Log in</Link>
                </>
            }
        >
            <form className={styles.form} onSubmit={onSubmit}>
                <TextField
                    id="name"
                    label="Name"
                    type="text"
                    autoComplete="name"
                    required
                    value={name}
                    onChange={e => setName(e.target.value)}
                />
                <TextField
                    id="nickname"
                    label="Nickname"
                    type="text"
                    autoComplete="username"
                    placeholder="letters, numbers, underscore"
                    required
                    value={nickname}
                    onChange={e => setNickname(e.target.value)}
                />
                <TextField
                    id="email"
                    label="Email"
                    type="email"
                    autoComplete="email"
                    required
                    value={email}
                    onChange={e => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                />
                <div className={styles.passwordField}>
                    <TextField
                        id="password"
                        label="Password"
                        type="password"
                        autoComplete="new-password"
                        required
                        value={password}
                        onChange={e => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                    />
                    <PasswordRequirements password={password} />
                </div>
                <div>
                    <TextField
                        id="confirm-password"
                        label="Repeat password"
                        type="password"
                        autoComplete="new-password"
                        required
                        value={confirmPassword}
                        onChange={e => setConfirmPassword(e.target.value)}
                    />
                    {!passwordsMatch && <p className={styles.mismatch}>Passwords don&apos;t match.</p>}
                </div>
                <label className={styles.agree}>
                    <input type="checkbox" required checked={agreed} onChange={e => setAgreed(e.target.checked)} />
                    <span>
                        I agree to the <Link to="/terms">Terms of Use</Link>, <Link to="/privacy">Privacy Policy</Link>,
                        and <Link to="/cookies">Cookie Policy</Link>.
                    </span>
                </label>
                <Button type="submit" variant="flat" fullWidth disabled={pending || !canSubmit}>
                    {pending ? 'Creating account…' : 'Create account'}
                </Button>
            </form>
        </SplitAuthLayout>
    );
}

FILE: src\pages\reset-password\index.tsx
============================================================
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { Link, useNavigate, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { SplitAuthLayout } from '../../widgets/split-auth';
import { PasswordField } from '../../shared/ui/PasswordField';
import { TextField } from '../../shared/ui/TextField';
import { Button } from '../../shared/ui/Button';
import { PasswordRequirements } from '../../shared/ui/PasswordRequirements';
import { isPasswordValid } from '../../shared/lib/passwordRules';
import { resetPassword, ApiError } from '../../shared/api/statsAuth';
import { useAuth } from '../../shared/auth/useAuth';
import styles from './index.module.scss';

// "Generate" (заполняет поле случайным сильным паролем) и переключатель
// видимости — именно здесь, в окне сброса пароля, как и просили: здесь
// человек впервые видит новый пароль и должен быть уверен, что ввёл то,
// что собирался, прежде чем он окажется только в менеджере паролей.
export function ResetPasswordPage() {
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const { refresh } = useAuth();
    const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
    const token = searchParams.get('token') ?? '';
    const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
    const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState('');
    const [totpCode, setTotpCode] = useState('');
    const [needsTotp, setNeedsTotp] = useState(false);
    const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const [pending, setPending] = useState(false);

    const canSubmit = isPasswordValid(password) && confirmPassword === password;

    async function submitReset(code?: string) {
        setError(null);
        setPending(true);
        try {
            await resetPassword(token, password, code);
            await refresh();
            navigate('/account');
        } catch (err) {
            if (err instanceof ApiError && err.code === 'reset_totp_required') {
                // Пароль и токен валидны, но на аккаунте включена 2FA — тот
                // же запрос нужно повторить с кодом, не начиная заново.
                setNeedsTotp(true);
                setError(null);
                return;
            }
            setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : 'Something went wrong — please try again.');
        } finally {
            setPending(false);
        }
    }

    function onSubmit(e: FormEvent) {
        e.preventDefault();
        void submitReset();
    }

    function onSubmitTotp(e: FormEvent) {
        e.preventDefault();
        void submitReset(totpCode.trim());
    }

    return (
        <SplitAuthLayout
            badge="Almost there"
            heading={
                <>
                    Set a new
                    <br />
                    <span className="hl">password.</span>
                </>
            }
            lead="Choose a new password for your account. You'll be signed in right after."
            cardTitle="New password"
            error={error}
            footer={
                <>
                    Link not working? <Link to="/forgot-password">Request a new one</Link>
                </>
            }
        >
            {!token ? (
                <p className={styles.missing}>
                    This link is missing its reset code. <Link to="/forgot-password">Request a new one</Link>.
                </p>
            ) : needsTotp ? (
                <form className={styles.form} onSubmit={onSubmitTotp}>
                    <p className={styles.missing}>
                        2FA is enabled on this account — enter a code from your authenticator app (or a recovery code)
                        to finish resetting your password.
                    </p>
                    <TextField
                        id="reset-totp-code"
                        label="2FA code"
                        type="text"
                        inputMode="numeric"
                        autoComplete="one-time-code"
                        required
                        value={totpCode}
                        onChange={e => setTotpCode(e.target.value)}
                    />
                    <Button type="submit" variant="flat" fullWidth disabled={pending || totpCode.trim().length < 6}>
                        {pending ? 'Verifying…' : 'Verify & set new password'}
                    </Button>
                </form>
            ) : (
                <form className={styles.form} onSubmit={onSubmit}>
                    <div className={styles.passwordField}>
                        <PasswordField
                            id="password"
                            label="New password"
                            autoComplete="new-password"
                            required
                            showGenerate
                            value={password}
                            onChange={setPassword}
                        />
                        <PasswordRequirements password={password} />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <PasswordField
                            id="confirm-password"
                            label="Repeat password"
                            autoComplete="new-password"
                            required
                            value={confirmPassword}
                            onChange={setConfirmPassword}
                        />
                        {confirmPassword.length > 0 && confirmPassword !== password && (
                            <p className={styles.mismatch}>Passwords don&apos;t match.</p>
                        )}
                    </div>
                    <Button type="submit" variant="flat" fullWidth disabled={pending || !canSubmit}>
                        {pending ? 'Saving…' : 'Set new password'}
                    </Button>
                </form>
            )}
        </SplitAuthLayout>
    );
}

============================================================
FILE: src\pages\riot-lookup\index.module.scss
============================================================
.form {
    display: flex;
    flex-direction: column;
    gap: 16px;
}

.result {
    margin-top: 20px;
    padding: 16px;
    background: var(--surface-2);
    border: 1px solid var(--border-strong);
    border-radius: 8px;
}

.resultTitle {
    font-size: 11px;
    font-weight: 700;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 0.06em;
    color: var(--win);
    margin-bottom: 8px;

FILE: src\pages\riot-lookup\index.tsx
============================================================
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { SplitAuthLayout } from '../../widgets/split-auth';
import { TextField } from '../../shared/ui/TextField';
import { Button } from '../../shared/ui/Button';
import { lookupRiotId, ApiError } from '../../shared/api/statsAuth';
import styles from './index.module.scss';

const RIOT_DISCLAIMER =
    "Extree Stats isn't endorsed by Riot Games and doesn't reflect the views or opinions of Riot Games or anyone officially involved in producing or managing VALORANT. VALORANT and Riot Games are trademarks or registered trademarks of Riot Games, Inc.";

// Пока показывает только "аккаунт существует" (account-v1) — Match V1
// (сами матчи) этому ключу не отдан, см. stats-backend/riotApi.js.
// Честно говорим об этом в lead и в карточке результата, а не притворяемся
// неработающей фичей или не показываем вовсе.
export function RiotLookupPage() {
    const [riotId, setRiotId] = useState('');
    const [result, setResult] = useState<{ gameName: string; tagLine: string } | null>(null);
    const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const [pending, setPending] = useState(false);

    async function onSubmit(e: FormEvent) {
        e.preventDefault();
        setError(null);
        setResult(null);
        setPending(true);
        try {
            const found = await lookupRiotId(riotId);
            setResult({ gameName: found.gameName, tagLine: found.tagLine });
        } catch (err) {
            setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : 'Something went wrong — please try again.');
        } finally {
            setPending(false);
        }
    }

    return (
        <SplitAuthLayout
            badge="VALORANT API — account lookup"
            heading={
                <>
                    Find your
                    <br />
                    <span className="hl">Riot ID.</span>
                </>
            }
            lead="We can already confirm a Riot account exists through Riot's API. Full match stats — K/D, win rates, maps — are coming once Riot approves full API access for Extree Stats."
            cardTitle="Look up a Riot ID"
            error={error}
            footer={
                <>
                    Looking for something else? <Link to="/">Back to Extree Stats</Link>
                </>
            }
        >
            <form className={styles.form} onSubmit={onSubmit}>
                <TextField
                    id="riotId"
                    label="Riot ID"
                    type="text"
                    placeholder="Name#Tag"
                    required
                    value={riotId}
                    onChange={e => setRiotId(e.target.value)}
                />
                <Button type="submit" variant="flat" fullWidth disabled={pending}>
                    {pending ? 'Looking up…' : 'Look up'}
                </Button>
            </form>

            {result && (
                <div className={styles.result}>
                    <div className={styles.resultTitle}>Account found</div>
                    <div className={styles.resultId}>
                        {result.gameName}
                        <span className={styles.resultTag}>#{result.tagLine}</span>
                    </div>
                    <p className={styles.resultNote}>
                        Match history and stats aren&apos;t available yet — Riot hasn&apos;t approved full API access
                        for Extree Stats.
                    </p>
                </div>
            )}

            <p className={styles.disclaimer}>{RIOT_DISCLAIMER}</p>
        </SplitAuthLayout>
    );
}

FILE: src\pages\sign-in\Faq.tsx
