FILE: src\widgets\admin-tickets-list\index.tsx
============================================================
import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { formatRelativeTime } from '../../shared/lib/formatRelativeTime';
import { Skeleton } from '../../shared/ui/Skeleton';
import { type TicketStatus, type TicketSummary } from '../../shared/api/statsAuth';
import styles from './index.module.scss';

const CATEGORY_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
    account: 'Account & login',
    bug: 'Bug report',
    billing: 'Billing',
    other: 'Other',
};

const STATUS_FILTERS: Array<{ key: '' | TicketStatus; label: string }> = [
    { key: '', label: 'All' },
    { key: 'open', label: 'Open' },
    { key: 'in_progress', label: 'In progress' },
    { key: 'closed', label: 'Closed' },
];

interface AdminTicketsListProps {
    tickets: TicketSummary[] | null;
    activeNumber: number | null;
    onSelect: (number: number) => void;
    status: '' | TicketStatus;
    onStatusChange: (status: '' | TicketStatus) => void;
}

export function AdminTicketsList({ tickets, activeNumber, onSelect, status, onStatusChange }: AdminTicketsListProps) {
    const [search, setSearch] = useState('');

    const counts = useMemo(() => {
        if (!tickets) return { total: 0, open: 0, in_progress: 0, closed: 0 };
        return {
            total: tickets.length,
            open: tickets.filter(t => t.status === 'open').length,
            in_progress: tickets.filter(t => t.status === 'in_progress').length,
            closed: tickets.filter(t => t.status === 'closed').length,
        };
    }, [tickets]);

    const visible = useMemo(() => {
        if (!tickets) return null;
        const q = search.trim().toLowerCase();
        if (!q) return tickets;
        return tickets.filter(t => t.subject.toLowerCase().includes(q) || t.email.toLowerCase().includes(q));
    }, [tickets, search]);

    function countFor(key: '' | TicketStatus): number {
        if (key === '') return counts.total;
        return counts[key];
    }

    return (
        <div className={styles.container}>
            <div className={styles.header}>
                <div className={styles.titleRow}>
                    <h1 className={styles.title}>Inbox</h1>
                    {tickets && <span className={styles.badge}>{counts.total}</span>}
                </div>

                <div className={styles.searchWrapper}>
                    <svg
                        className={styles.searchIcon}
                        viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                        fill="none"
                        stroke="currentColor"
                        strokeWidth="2"
                        strokeLinecap="round"
                        strokeLinejoin="round"
                        aria-hidden="true"
                    >
                        <circle cx="11" cy="11" r="8" />
                        <line x1="21" y1="21" x2="16.65" y2="16.65" />
                    </svg>
                    <input
                        type="search"
                        className={styles.searchInput}
                        placeholder="Search by subject or email…"
                        value={search}
                        onChange={e => setSearch(e.target.value)}
                    />
                </div>

                <nav className={styles.filters} aria-label="Filter tickets by status">
                    {STATUS_FILTERS.map(f => (
                        <button
                            key={f.key || 'all'}
                            type="button"
                            className={`${styles.filterBtn} ${status === f.key ? styles.filterBtnActive : ''}`}
                            onClick={() => onStatusChange(f.key)}
                        >
                            <span>{f.label}</span>
                            <span className={styles.filterCount}>{countFor(f.key)}</span>
                        </button>
                    ))}
                </nav>
            </div>

            <div className={styles.scrollArea}>
                {tickets === null && <TicketListSkeleton />}
                {visible && visible.length === 0 && (
                    <div className={styles.placeholder}>
                        {search.trim() ? 'No tickets match your search.' : 'No tickets in this filter.'}
                    </div>
                )}
                {visible && visible.length > 0 && (
                    <ul className={styles.list}>
                        {visible.map(t => (
                            <li key={t.number}>
                                <button
                                    type="button"
                                    className={`${styles.card} ${activeNumber === t.number ? styles.cardActive : ''}`}
                                    onClick={() => onSelect(t.number)}
                                >
                                    <div className={styles.cardTop}>
                                        <StatusDot status={t.status} />
                                        <span className={styles.number}>#{t.number}</span>
                                        <span className={styles.time}>{formatRelativeTime(t.time)}</span>
                                    </div>
                                    <div className={styles.subject}>{t.subject}</div>
                                    <div className={styles.meta}>
                                        <span className={styles.email}>{t.email}</span>
                                        <span className={styles.category}>
                                            {CATEGORY_LABELS[t.category] ?? t.category}
                                        </span>
                                    </div>
                                </button>
                            </li>
                        ))}
                    </ul>
                )}
            </div>
        </div>
    );
}

function StatusDot({ status }: { status: TicketStatus }) {
    return <span className={`${styles.dot} ${styles[`dot_${status}`]}`} aria-hidden="true" />;
}

// Skeleton для левой колонки — 5 placeholder-карточек, чтобы layout не
// "прыгал" при загрузке (высота карточки ~60px + отступы).
function TicketListSkeleton() {
    return (
        <div className={styles.skeletonList} aria-hidden="true">
            {Array.from({ length: 5 }).map((_, i) => (
                <div key={i} className={styles.skeletonCard}>
                    <Skeleton width="40%" height={10} radius={3} />
                    <Skeleton width="90%" height={14} radius={4} />
                    <Skeleton width="60%" height={11} radius={3} />
                </div>
            ))}
        </div>
    );
}

import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { SiteHeader } from '../site-header';
import styles from './index.module.scss';

interface AuthCardProps {
    title: string;
    subtitle?: string;
    children: ReactNode;
    footer?: ReactNode;
    error?: string | null;
}

// Общая обёртка для register/login/verify — те же токены и структура,
// что у остального продукта (SiteHeader, мягкое ambient-свечение из
// pages/sign-in), просто центрированная карточка вместо двухколоночной
// раскладки — под короткую форму это уместнее.
export function AuthCard({ title, subtitle, children, footer, error }: AuthCardProps) {
    return (
        <div className={styles.page}>
            <div className={styles.ambient} aria-hidden="true" />
            <SiteHeader />
            <main className={styles.stage}>
                <div className={styles.card}>
                    <h1 className={styles.title}>{title}</h1>
                    {subtitle && <p className={styles.subtitle}>{subtitle}</p>}
                    {error && (
                        <p className={styles.error} role="alert">
                            {error}
                        </p>
                    )}
                    {children}
                    {footer && <div className={styles.footer}>{footer}</div>}
                </div>
            </main>
        </div>
    );
}

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { getCannedResponses, type CannedResponse } from '../../shared/api/statsAuth';
import styles from './index.module.scss';

interface CannedTemplatePickerProps {
    onPick: (body: string) => void;
    disabled?: boolean;
}

// SRP: компонент только выбирает шаблон и отдаёт его через onPick.
// CRUD шаблонов (создание/удаление) — отдельная страница в следующей
// итерации. Здесь же — только "вставить существующий", как в Zendesk/
// Intercom (кнопка Insert template ▾ над textarea).
export function CannedTemplatePicker({ onPick, disabled }: CannedTemplatePickerProps) {
    const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
    const [templates, setTemplates] = useState<CannedResponse[] | null>(null);
    const [loadError, setLoadError] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!open || templates !== null) return;
        getCannedResponses()
            .then(result => setTemplates(result.responses))
            .catch(() => setLoadError('Failed to load templates'));
    }, [open, templates]);

    useEffect(() => {
        function handleClickOutside(event: MouseEvent) {
            if (containerRef.current && !containerRef.current.contains(event.target as Node)) {
                setOpen(false);
            }
        }
        if (open) {
            document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
            return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
        }
    }, [open]);

    function handlePick(body: string) {
        onPick(body);
        setOpen(false);
    }

    return (
        <div className={styles.wrapper} ref={containerRef}>
            <button
                type="button"
                className={styles.trigger}
                onClick={() => setOpen(v => !v)}
                disabled={disabled}
                aria-haspopup="listbox"
                aria-expanded={open}
            >
                <span>Insert template</span>
                <svg
                    className={`${styles.caret} ${open ? styles.caretOpen : ''}`}
                    viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                    fill="none"
                    stroke="currentColor"
                    strokeWidth="2"
                    strokeLinecap="round"
                    strokeLinejoin="round"
                >
                    <polyline points="6 9 12 15 18 9" />
                </svg>
            </button>

            {open && (
                <div className={styles.menu} role="listbox">
                    {loadError && <div className={styles.error}>{loadError}</div>}
                    {templates === null && !loadError && <div className={styles.empty}>Loading…</div>}
                    {templates?.length === 0 && <div className={styles.empty}>No templates yet.</div>}
                    {templates && templates.length > 0 && (
                        <ul className={styles.list}>
                            {templates.map(t => (
                                <li key={t.id}>
                                    <button type="button" className={styles.item} onClick={() => handlePick(t.body)}>
                                        <span className={styles.itemTitle}>{t.title}</span>
                                        <span className={styles.itemBody}>{t.body}</span>
                                    </button>
                                </li>
                            ))}
                        </ul>
                    )}
                </div>
            )}
        </div>
    );
}

FILE: src\widgets\command-palette\index.tsx
============================================================
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { globalSearch, type GlobalSearchResult } from '../../shared/api/statsAuth';
import { useKeyboardShortcut } from '../../shared/lib/useKeyboardShortcut';
import styles from './index.module.scss';

/**
 * Cmd+K палитра. Ctrl+K / Cmd+K открывает, Esc закрывает.
 * Ищет по тикетам и юзерам через /api/admin/search. Enter — переход.
 * Доступна только админам (проверяется на бэке — 403 → пустой результат).
 */
export function CommandPalette() {
    const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
    const [q, setQ] = useState('');
    const [result, setResult] = useState<GlobalSearchResult>({ tickets: [], users: [] });
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
    const [activeIdx, setActiveIdx] = useState(0);
    const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
    const navigate = useNavigate();

    useKeyboardShortcut('k', true, () => setOpen(o => !o));

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!open) return;
        const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
            if (e.key === 'Escape') setOpen(false);
        };
        document.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
        return () => document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    }, [open]);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (open) setTimeout(() => inputRef.current?.focus(), 50);
        else setQ('');
    }, [open]);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (q.trim().length < 2) {
            setResult({ tickets: [], users: [] });
            return;
        }
        let cancelled = false;
        setLoading(true);
        const t = setTimeout(() => {
            globalSearch(q)
                .then(r => {
                    if (!cancelled) setResult(r);
                })
                .catch(() => {
                    if (!cancelled) setResult({ tickets: [], users: [] });
                })
                .finally(() => {
                    if (!cancelled) setLoading(false);
                });
        }, 200);
        return () => {
            cancelled = true;
            clearTimeout(t);
        };
    }, [q]);

    const items = useMemo(() => {
        const list: { label: string; sub: string; to: string }[] = [];
        for (const t of result.tickets) {
            list.push({ label: `#${t.number} ${t.subject}`, sub: t.email, to: `/admin/tickets/${t.number}` });
        }
        for (const u of result.users) {
            list.push({ label: u.name || u.email, sub: u.email, to: `/admin/users?q=${encodeURIComponent(u.email)}` });
        }
        return list;
    }, [result]);

    useEffect(() => {
        setActiveIdx(0);
    }, [items.length]);

    function onPick(to: string) {
        navigate(to);
        setOpen(false);
    }

    if (!open) return null;

    return (
        <div className={styles.backdrop} onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>
            <div className={styles.modal} onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()}>
                <input
                    ref={inputRef}
                    className={styles.input}
                    placeholder="Search tickets or users…"
                    value={q}
                    onChange={e => setQ(e.target.value)}
                    onKeyDown={e => {
                        if (e.key === 'ArrowDown') {
                            e.preventDefault();
                            setActiveIdx(i => Math.min(i + 1, items.length - 1));
                        }
                        if (e.key === 'ArrowUp') {
                            e.preventDefault();
                            setActiveIdx(i => Math.max(i - 1, 0));
                        }
                        if (e.key === 'Enter' && items[activeIdx]) onPick(items[activeIdx].to);
                    }}
                />
                <div className={styles.results}>
                    {loading && <div className={styles.empty}>Searching…</div>}
                    {!loading && q.length >= 2 && items.length === 0 && (
                        <div className={styles.empty}>No results for "{q}"</div>
                    )}
                    {!loading && q.length < 2 && <div className={styles.empty}>Type at least 2 characters.</div>}
                    {items.map((item, i) => (
                        <button
                            key={i}
                            className={`${styles.item} ${i === activeIdx ? styles.active : ''}`}
                            onMouseEnter={() => setActiveIdx(i)}
                            onClick={() => onPick(item.to)}
                        >
                            <span className={styles.label}>{item.label}</span>
                            <span className={styles.sub}>{item.sub}</span>
                        </button>
                    ))}
                </div>
                <div className={styles.hint}>
                    <kbd>↑↓</kbd> navigate · <kbd>↵</kbd> open · <kbd>esc</kbd> close
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
}

FILE: src\widgets\cookie-consent\index.tsx

import { useState } from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Button } from '../../shared/ui/Button';
import { useConsent } from '../../shared/consent/useConsent';
import styles from './index.module.scss';

// Показывается, пока consent === null (решение ещё не принято) — см.
// ConsentProvider. "Customize" разворачивает три категории инлайн, без
// отдельной модалки: Necessary (всегда включено, сессия/CSRF/Turnstile —
// без них сайт не работает), Analytics (Sentry) и Preferences (Google
// Fonts) — обе non-essential, по умолчанию выключены до явного согласия.
export function CookieConsentBanner() {
    const { bannerOpen, acceptAll, rejectNonEssential, savePreferences, consent } = useConsent();
    const [customizing, setCustomizing] = useState(false);
    const [analytics, setAnalytics] = useState(consent?.analytics ?? false);
    const [preferences, setPreferences] = useState(consent?.preferences ?? false);

    if (!bannerOpen) return null;

    return (
        <div className={styles.banner} role="dialog" aria-label="Cookie preferences" aria-modal="false">
            <div className={styles.inner}>
                {!customizing ? (
                    <>
                        <p className={styles.text}>
                            We use strictly necessary cookies to keep you signed in and the site secure. With your
                            consent, we&apos;d also like to use error monitoring and load the Inter typeface from Google
                            Fonts — see our <Link to="/cookies">Cookie Policy</Link> for the full breakdown.
                        </p>
                        <div className={styles.actions}>
                            <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={() => setCustomizing(true)}>
                                Customize
                            </Button>
                            <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={rejectNonEssential}>
                                Necessary only
                            </Button>
                            <Button variant="primary" size="sm" onClick={acceptAll}>
                                Accept all
                            </Button>
                        </div>
                    </>
                ) : (
                    <>
                        <div className={styles.categories}>
                            <label className={styles.category}>
                                <input type="checkbox" checked disabled aria-label="Necessary (always on)" />
                                <span>
                                    <strong>Necessary</strong>
                                    <p>
                                        Session, CSRF protection, and bot-protection (Cloudflare Turnstile) on sign-in.
                                        Always on — the site can&apos;t function without these.
                                    </p>
                                </span>
                            </label>
                            <label className={styles.category}>
                                <input
                                    type="checkbox"
                                    checked={analytics}
                                    onChange={e => setAnalytics(e.target.checked)}
                                    aria-label="Error monitoring"
                                />
                                <span>
                                    <strong>Error monitoring</strong>
                                    <p>
                                        Sentry reports crashes so we can fix them. Sends your browser info and IP to
                                        Sentry only when something breaks.
                                    </p>
                                </span>
                            </label>
                            <label className={styles.category}>
                                <input
                                    type="checkbox"
                                    checked={preferences}
                                    onChange={e => setPreferences(e.target.checked)}
                                    aria-label="Fonts"
                                />
                                <span>
                                    <strong>Fonts</strong>
                                    <p>
                                        Loads the Inter typeface from Google Fonts. Declining just falls back to your
                                        system font.
                                    </p>
                                </span>
                            </label>
                        </div>
                        <div className={styles.actions}>
                            <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={() => setCustomizing(false)}>
                                Back
                            </Button>
                            <Button
                                variant="primary"
                                size="sm"
                                onClick={() => savePreferences({ analytics, preferences })}
                            >
                                Save preferences
                            </Button>
                        </div>
                    </>
                )}
            </div>
        </div>
    );
}

FILE: src\widgets\dashboard-shell\index.tsx
============================================================
import { useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { Link, NavLink, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Avatar } from '../../shared/ui/Avatar';
import { HomeIcon, SettingsIcon, TicketIcon, ShieldIcon, UsersIcon, PowerIcon, MenuIcon, IconX, UserIcon, BellIcon } from '../../shared/ui/Icons';
import styles from './index.module.scss';

export function DashboardShell({ user, breadcrumb, title, subtitle, headerExtra, error, wide, children }: any) {
    const [sidebarOpen, setSidebarOpen] = useState(false);
    const [settingsOpen, setSettingsOpen] = useState(false);
    const location = useLocation();
    const isSettingsActive = location.pathname.includes('/account/settings');
    const signOut = () => { window.location.href = '/auth/logout'; };
    const navClass = ({ isActive }: { isActive: boolean }) => `${styles.navLink} ${isActive ? styles.active : ''}`;

    return (
        <div className={styles.shell}>
            <button className={styles.mobileToggle} onClick={() => setSidebarOpen(true)}><MenuIcon /></button>
            {sidebarOpen && <div className={styles.sidebarBackdrop} onClick={() => setSidebarOpen(false)} />}

            <aside className={`${styles.sidebar} ${sidebarOpen ? styles.sidebarOpen : ''}`}>
                <div className={styles.sidebarHeader}>
                    <Link to="/" className={styles.brand}>
                        <img src="/logo.png" alt="" className={styles.mark} />
                        <span>EXTREE</span>
                    </Link>
                    <button className={styles.closeSidebar} onClick={() => setSidebarOpen(false)}><IconX /></button>
                </div>

                {user && (
                    <nav className={styles.nav}>
                        <NavLink to="/account" end className={navClass} onClick={() => setSidebarOpen(false)}>
                            <HomeIcon /> Dashboard
                        </NavLink>

                        <div className={styles.navGroup}>
                            <button className={`${styles.navLink} ${isSettingsActive ? styles.active : ''}`} onClick={() => setSettingsOpen(o => !o)}>
                                <SettingsIcon /> Settings
                                <span className={`${styles.chevron} ${settingsOpen ? styles.chevronOpen : ''}`}><svg width="12" height="12" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2"><path d="m6 9 6 6 6-6"/></svg></span>
                            </button>
                            <div className={`${styles.subMenu} ${settingsOpen ? styles.subMenuOpen : ''}`}>
                                <div>
                                    <NavLink to="/account/settings" end className={navClass} onClick={() => setSidebarOpen(false)}><UserIcon /> Profile</NavLink>
                                    <NavLink to="/account/settings?tab=security" className={navClass} onClick={() => setSidebarOpen(false)}><ShieldIcon /> Security</NavLink>
                                    <NavLink to="/account/settings?tab=notifications" className={navClass} onClick={() => setSidebarOpen(false)}><BellIcon /> Notifications</NavLink>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <NavLink to="/support" className={navClass} onClick={() => setSidebarOpen(false)}><TicketIcon /> Support</NavLink>

                        {user.isAdmin && (
                            <>
                                <div className={styles.navLabel}>Admin Area</div>
                                <NavLink to="/admin/users" className={navClass} onClick={() => setSidebarOpen(false)}><UsersIcon /> Manage Users</NavLink>
                                <NavLink to="/admin/tickets" className={navClass} onClick={() => setSidebarOpen(false)}><TicketIcon /> Tickets</NavLink>
                                <NavLink to="/admin/audit" className={navClass} onClick={() => setSidebarOpen(false)}><ShieldIcon /> Audit Log</NavLink>
                            </>
                        )}
                    </nav>
                )}

                {user && (
                    <div className={styles.sidebarFooter}>
                        <div className={styles.userChip}>
                            <Avatar label={user.email} />
                            <span className={styles.userEmail}>{user.email}</span>
                        </div>
                        <button className={styles.signOutBtn} onClick={signOut}><PowerIcon /></button>
                    </div>
                )}
            </aside>

            <main className={styles.main}>
                <div className={`${styles.content} ${wide ? styles.contentWide : ''}`}>
                    <div className={styles.pageHead}>
                        <div>
                            {breadcrumb && <Link to={breadcrumb.to} className={styles.breadcrumb}>← {breadcrumb.label}</Link>}
                            <h1 className={styles.pageTitle}>{title}</h1>
                            {subtitle && <p className={styles.pageSubtitle}>{subtitle}</p>}
                        </div>
                        {headerExtra}
                    </div>
                    {error && <p className={styles.error}>{error}</p>}
                    {children}
                </div>
            </main>
        </div>
    );
}

FILE: src\widgets\demo-dashboard\index.tsx
============================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useMediaQuery } from '../../shared/lib/useMediaQuery';
import { Skeleton } from '../../shared/ui/Skeleton';
import { TrophyIcon } from '../../shared/ui/Icons';
import { MAP_WIN_RATES, KD_TREND, RANK, TOP_AGENTS, winRate } from './data';
import { useCountUp } from './useCountUp';
import styles from './index.module.scss';

const REDUCED_MOTION_QUERY = '(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)';

function MapBar({
    map,
    wins,
    losses,
    loaded,
    animate,
}: {
    map: string;
    wins: number;
    losses: number;
    loaded: boolean;
    animate: boolean;
}) {
    const rate = winRate({ map, wins, losses });
    const tooltipId = `maprate-${map.toLowerCase()}`;

    return (
        <div className={styles.barRow}>
            <span className={styles.barLabel}>{map}</span>
            <span className={styles.barTrack}>
                <button
                    type="button"
                    className={styles.barFill}
                    style={{ width: loaded ? `${rate}%` : 0, transitionDuration: animate ? '700ms' : '0ms' }}
                    aria-describedby={tooltipId}
                >
                    <span className={styles.barTooltip} id={tooltipId} role="tooltip">
                        {map} — {rate}% ({wins} wins of {wins + losses})
                    </span>
                </button>
            </span>
            <span className={styles.barValue}>{loaded ? `${rate}%` : ''}</span>
        </div>
    );
}

function KdSparkline({ loaded, animate }: { loaded: boolean; animate: boolean }) {
    const w = 220;
    const h = 56;
    const max = Math.max(...KD_TREND);
    const min = Math.min(...KD_TREND);
    const span = max - min || 1;
    const points = KD_TREND.map((v, i) => {
        const x = (i / (KD_TREND.length - 1)) * w;
        const y = h - ((v - min) / span) * h;
        return `${x.toFixed(1)},${y.toFixed(1)}`;
    }).join(' ');
    const pathLength = w * 1.4; // приблизительная длина ломаной, достаточно для dash-анимации

    return (
        <svg
            className={styles.kdChart}
            viewBox={`0 0 ${w} ${h}`}
            width={w}
            height={h}
            role="img"
            aria-labelledby="kd-chart-title"
        >
            <title id="kd-chart-title">K/D trend over the last {KD_TREND.length} matches, demo data</title>
            <polyline
                points={points}
                fill="none"
                stroke="var(--accent)"
                strokeWidth="2"
                strokeLinecap="round"
                strokeLinejoin="round"
                style={{
                    strokeDasharray: pathLength,
                    strokeDashoffset: loaded ? 0 : pathLength,
                    transition: animate ? 'stroke-dashoffset 900ms ease-out' : 'none',
                }}
            />
        </svg>
    );
}

function AgentCard({
    name,
    games,
    wins,
    loaded,
    animate,
}: {
    name: string;
    games: number;
    wins: number;
    loaded: boolean;
    animate: boolean;
}) {
    const rate = Math.round((wins / games) * 100);
    const animatedRate = useCountUp(rate, loaded && animate);

    return (
        <div className={styles.agentCard}>
            <span className={styles.agentAvatar} aria-hidden="true">
                {name.slice(0, 1)}
            </span>
            <span className={styles.agentName}>{name}</span>
            <span className={styles.agentRate}>{loaded ? `${animatedRate}%` : ''}</span>
            <span className={styles.agentGames}>{games} games</span>
        </div>
    );
}

// Витрина продукта на лендинге — не иллюстрация, а мини-копия реального
// дашборда с демо-данными (см. data.ts). Загрузка здесь не сетевая (данные
// статические), поэтому "loading" — это только эмуляция первого рендера для
// анимации появления; состояний error/empty нет и быть не может, раз нет
// запроса, который мог бы упасть.
export function DemoDashboard() {
    const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState(false);
    const [showAllAgents, setShowAllAgents] = useState(false);
    const isMobile = useMediaQuery('(max-width: 640px)');
    const reducedMotion = useMediaQuery(REDUCED_MOTION_QUERY);
    const animate = !reducedMotion;

    useEffect(() => {
        if (reducedMotion) {
            setLoaded(true);
            return;
        }
        const t = window.setTimeout(() => setLoaded(true), 450);
        return () => window.clearTimeout(t);
    }, [reducedMotion]);

    const visibleAgents = isMobile && !showAllAgents ? TOP_AGENTS.slice(0, 2) : TOP_AGENTS;

    return (
        <div className={styles.dashboard} id="demo-dashboard">
            <div className={styles.dashboardHead}>
                <span className={styles.dashboardTitle}>Your dashboard</span>
                <span className={styles.demoLabel}>Demo data</span>
            </div>

            <section className={styles.panel} aria-label="Win rate by map">
                <h3 className={styles.panelTitle}>Win rate by map</h3>
                {loaded ? (
                    <div className={styles.barList}>
                        {MAP_WIN_RATES.map(row => (
                            <MapBar key={row.map} {...row} loaded={loaded} animate={animate} />
                        ))}
                    </div>
                ) : (
                    <div className={styles.barList}>
                        {MAP_WIN_RATES.map(row => (
                            <div className={styles.barRow} key={row.map}>
                                <Skeleton width={46} height={12} />
                                <Skeleton width="100%" height={10} radius={999} className={styles.barSkeletonTrack} />
                            </div>
                        ))}
                    </div>
                )}
            </section>

            <div className={styles.panelGrid}>
                <section className={styles.panel} aria-label="K/D trend">
                    <h3 className={styles.panelTitle}>K/D trend</h3>
                    {loaded ? <KdSparkline loaded={loaded} animate={animate} /> : <Skeleton width={220} height={56} />}
                </section>

                <section className={styles.panel} aria-label="Rank progress">
                    <h3 className={styles.panelTitle}>Rank progress</h3>
                    {loaded ? (
                        <div className={styles.rankBlock}>
                            <span className={styles.rankBadge}>
                                <TrophyIcon />
                                {RANK.tier}
                            </span>
                            <div className={styles.rankTrack}>
                                <div
                                    className={styles.rankFill}
                                    style={{
                                        width: `${(RANK.rr / RANK.rrToPromo) * 100}%`,
                                        transitionDuration: animate ? '700ms' : '0ms',
                                    }}
                                />
                            </div>
                            <span className={styles.rankText}>
                                {RANK.rr} / {RANK.rrToPromo} RR to promotion
                            </span>
                        </div>
                    ) : (
                        <div className={styles.rankBlock}>
                            <Skeleton width={120} height={24} radius={999} />
                            <Skeleton width="100%" height={8} radius={999} />
                        </div>
                    )}
                </section>
            </div>

            <section className={styles.panel} aria-label="Top agents">
                <h3 className={styles.panelTitle}>Top agents</h3>
                {loaded ? (
                    <>
                        <div className={styles.agentGrid}>
                            {visibleAgents.map(agent => (
                                <AgentCard key={agent.name} {...agent} loaded={loaded} animate={animate} />
                            ))}
                        </div>
                        {isMobile && !showAllAgents && TOP_AGENTS.length > visibleAgents.length && (
                            <button type="button" className={styles.showMore} onClick={() => setShowAllAgents(true)}>
                                Show {TOP_AGENTS.length - visibleAgents.length} more
                            </button>
                        )}
                    </>
                ) : (
                    <div className={styles.agentGrid}>
                        {TOP_AGENTS.slice(0, isMobile ? 2 : 4).map(agent => (
                            <Skeleton key={agent.name} width="100%" height={84} radius={8} />
                        ))}
                    </div>
                )}
            </section>
        </div>
    );
}

FILE: src\widgets\site-header\index.tsx
============================================================
import { type ReactNode, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Button } from '../../shared/ui/Button';
import { ProfileDropdown } from '../../shared/ui/ProfileDropdown';
import { MenuIcon, IconX } from '../../shared/ui/Icons';
import { useAuth } from '../../shared/auth/useAuth';
import styles from './index.module.scss';

export function SiteHeader({ rightSlot, actions, navLinks }: any) {
    const { status, user } = useAuth();
    const [scrolled, setScrolled] = useState(false);
    const [menuOpen, setMenuOpen] = useState(false);

    useEffect(() => {
        const onScroll = () => setScrolled(window.scrollY > 20);
        window.addEventListener('scroll', onScroll);
        return () => window.removeEventListener('scroll', onScroll);
    }, []);

    const showDefaultActions = rightSlot === undefined && actions === undefined;
    const actionsContent = showDefaultActions ? (
        <div className={styles.actions}>
            {status === 'authenticated' && user ? (
                <>
                    <Button href="/account" variant="primary" size="sm">Dashboard</Button>
                    <ProfileDropdown email={user.email} isAdmin={user.isAdmin} />
                </>
            ) : (
                <>
                    <Button href="/login" variant="ghost" size="sm">Log in</Button>
                    <Button href="/register" variant="primary" size="sm">Sign up</Button>
                </>
            )}
        </div>
    ) : (rightSlot || actions);

    return (
        <header className={`${styles.header} ${scrolled ? styles.scrolled : ''}`}>
            <div className={styles.inner}>
                <Link to="/" className={styles.brand}>
                    <img src="/logo.png" alt="" className={styles.mark} />
                    <span>EXTREE</span>
                </Link>

                {navLinks && (
                    <nav className={styles.navLinks}>
                        {navLinks.map((link: any) => (
                            <a key={link.href} href={link.href} className={styles.navLink}>{link.label}</a>
                        ))}
                    </nav>
                )}

                <div className={styles.navRight}>
                    {actionsContent}
                    {navLinks && (
                        <button className={styles.menuToggle} onClick={() => setMenuOpen(o => !o)}>
                            {menuOpen ? <IconX /> : <MenuIcon />}
                        </button>
                    )}
                </div>
            </div>
        </header>
    );
}

FILE: src\widgets\split-auth\index.tsx

import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import styles from './index.module.scss';

interface SplitAuthLayoutProps {
    badge: ReactNode;
    heading: ReactNode;
    lead?: ReactNode;
    leftExtra?: ReactNode;
    cardTitle: string;
    error?: string | null;
    children: ReactNode;
    footer?: ReactNode;
}

// Форма по центру, без хедера. Поля — полоска снизу, кнопки — капсулы.
export function SplitAuthLayout({
    badge,
    heading,
    lead,
    leftExtra,
    cardTitle,
    error,
    children,
    footer,
}: SplitAuthLayoutProps) {
    return (
        <div className={styles.page}>
            <main className={styles.main}>
                <div className={styles.sheet}>
                    <div className={styles.badge} aria-hidden="true">
                        {badge}
                    </div>
                    <h1 className={styles.heading}>{heading}</h1>
                    {lead && <p className={styles.lead}>{lead}</p>}
                    {leftExtra && <div className={styles.leftExtra}>{leftExtra}</div>}
                    <div className={styles.card}>
                        <h2 className={styles.cardTitle}>{cardTitle}</h2>
                        {error && (
                            <p className={styles.error} role="alert">
                                {error}
                            </p>
                        )}
                        {children}
                        {footer && <div className={styles.footer}>{footer}</div>}
                    </div>
                </div>
            </main>
        </div>
    );
}

FILE: src\widgets\ticket-thread\index.tsx
============================================================
import { Avatar } from '../../shared/ui/Avatar';
import { formatRelativeTime } from '../../shared/lib/formatRelativeTime';
import { Skeleton } from '../../shared/ui/Skeleton';
import styles from './index.module.scss';

interface ThreadMessage {
    id: string;
    isSupport: boolean;
    authorLabel: string;
    message: string;
    time: string;
}

interface TicketThreadProps {
    messages: ThreadMessage[];
}

// Comment-style thread (Linear / GitHub Issues): без пузырей, только
// Avatar + author + relative time + свободный текст. Используется и в
// админке, и в публичной странице тикета.
export function TicketThread({ messages }: TicketThreadProps) {
    if (messages.length === 0) {
        return <ThreadSkeleton />;
    }
    return (
        <div className={styles.thread}>
            {messages.map(m => (
                <article key={m.id} className={`${styles.comment} ${m.isSupport ? styles.commentSupport : ''}`}>
                    <Avatar label={m.authorLabel} isSupport={m.isSupport} />
                    <div className={styles.body}>
                        <header className={styles.head}>
                            <span className={styles.author}>{m.authorLabel}</span>
                            <span className={styles.time}>{formatRelativeTime(m.time)}</span>
                        </header>
                        <div className={styles.message}>{m.message}</div>
                    </div>
                </article>
            ))}
        </div>
    );
}

function ThreadSkeleton() {
    return (
        <div className={styles.thread} aria-hidden="true">
            {[0, 1, 2].map(i => (
                <article key={i} className={styles.comment}>
                    <Skeleton width={32} height={32} radius="50%" />
                    <div className={styles.body}>
                        <div className={styles.skeletonHead}>
                            <Skeleton width={80} height={12} radius={3} />
                            <Skeleton width={48} height={10} radius={3} />
                        </div>
                        <Skeleton width="100%" height={14} radius={4} />
                        <div style={{ height: 8 }} />
                        <Skeleton width="80%" height={14} radius={4} />
                    </div>
                </article>
            ))}
        </div>
    );
}
